  price_version varchar [note: 'Version number of the MSRP record for change tracking.']
  record_audit_created timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the MSRP record was first created in the lakehouse.']
  record_audit_updated timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the MSRP record.']

  Note: 'Manages the MSRP (Manufacturer Suggested Retail Price) schedule for vehicle configurations, trim levels, and option packages by market and MY. Captures base MSRP, destination and delivery charge, gas guzzler tax (if applicable), federal EV tax credit eligibility, effective date, expiry date, currency, and market region. Provides the authoritative pricing reference for dealer ordering, window sticker generation, and sales analytics. Distinct from dealer invoice pricing (owned by billing domain).'
}

Table vehicle.lifecycle_event {
  lifecycle_event_id varchar [pk, note: 'System-generated unique identifier for each vehicle lifecycle event record.']
  connected_vehicle_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to customer.connected_vehicle. Business justification: Lifecycle events triggered by telematics systems (OTA updates, connectivity activations, remote diagnostics) must reference the connected_vehicle record for connected mobility audit trails, OTA compli']
  dealership_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to dealer.dealership. Business justification: Vehicle lifecycle events occurring at a dealership need a dealership_id FK to attribute service, warranty, and recall actions to the correct dealer.']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.plant. Business justification: Required for compliance reports: each lifecycle event must record the employee who logged/handled it, ensuring traceability and auditability.']
  storage_location_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to inventory.storage_location. Business justification: Vehicle yard management and logistics tracking requires linking lifecycle events (line-off, yard move, PDI, delivery) to specific inventory storage locations. Automotive yard management systems track ']
  vehicle_compound_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to logistics.vehicle_compound. Business justification: Vehicle lifecycle events include compound arrivals, PDI completions, and compound departures — standard OEM outbound logistics milestones. Linking lifecycle_event to vehicle_compound enables compound ']
  vehicle_order_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.vehicle_order. Business justification: Lifecycle events (line-off, PDI completion, delivery handover) are milestones in order fulfillment. Linking lifecycle_event to vehicle_order enables order status tracking, customer delivery milestone ']
  vin_registry_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.vin_registry. Business justification: Vehicle lifecycle events reference a VIN; linking to vin_registry provides a stable surrogate and removes the raw VIN column.']
  event_category varchar [note: 'High‑level grouping of the event (e.g., production, logistics, sales, post‑sale, disposal). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: production|logistics|sales|post_sale|disposal — promote to reference product]']
  event_description varchar [note: 'Free‑text narrative describing the event details and context.']
  event_location_city varchar [note: 'City name where the event was recorded.']
  event_location_country varchar [note: 'Three‑letter ISO country code where the event took place.']
  event_location_latitude double [note: 'Geographic latitude of the event location in decimal degrees.']
  event_location_longitude double [note: 'Geographic longitude of the event location in decimal degrees.']
  event_location_state varchar [note: 'State or province abbreviation for the event location, if applicable.']
  event_notes varchar [note: 'Additional remarks or internal notes attached to the event.']
  event_sequence_number varchar [note: 'Sequential order of events for the same VIN, starting at 1.']
  event_source_module varchar [note: 'Specific module or component within the source system that emitted the event.']
  event_source_reference varchar [note: 'Unique identifier of the source system instance that emitted the event (e.g., message ID, log ID).']
  event_source_system varchar [note: 'Higher‑level system or application that originated the event record.']
  event_status varchar [note: 'Processing status of the event record within the data pipeline.']
  event_subtype varchar [note: 'More granular classification within the event category.']
  event_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date‑time when the lifecycle event actually occurred.']
  event_type varchar [note: 'Category of lifecycle transition (e.g., manufactured, pdi_completed, shipped_to_dealer, sold_retail, sold_fleet, exported, titled, re_acquired, scrapped, stolen, total_loss). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: manufactured|pdi_completed|shipped_to_dealer|sold_retail|sold_fleet|exported|titled|re_acquired|scrapped|stolen|total_loss — promote to reference product]']
  is_critical boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the event is considered critical for compliance or safety reporting.']
  odometer_reading_km varchar [note: 'Vehicle mileage at the time of the event, expressed in kilometers.']
  record_created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the event record was first inserted into the lakehouse.']
  record_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the event record.']
  triggering_system varchar [note: 'Name of the IT/OT system that generated the event (e.g., MES, DMS, Telematics Platform).']

  Note: 'Transactional log of significant lifecycle state transitions for a specific VIN throughout its life. Captures event type (manufactured, PDI-completed, shipped-to-dealer, sold-retail, sold-fleet, exported, titled, re-acquired, scrapped, stolen, total-loss), event timestamp, event location, responsible party, odometer reading at event, and triggering system. Provides the complete lifecycle audit trail for a vehicle unit from production to end-of-life, supporting title history, recall tracking, and residual value analytics.'
}

Table vehicle.ownership {
  ownership_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for the vehicle_ownership association']
  dealership_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to dealer.dealership. Business justification: Warranty registration and recall notification routing require knowing which dealer sold/transferred the vehicle. The selling dealership on the ownership record is used in OEM recall campaigns and warr']
  party_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to party']
  vehicle_order_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.vehicle_order. Business justification: Ownership records are created at point of sale. Linking ownership to the originating vehicle_order supports title transfer documentation, warranty registration initiation, and sales-to-ownership audit']
  vin_registry_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vin_registry']
  acquisition_date date [note: 'Date the party acquired the vehicle']
  disposition_date date [note: 'Date the party disposed of the vehicle']
  ownership_number varchar [note: 'Identifier for the ownership contract or agreement']
  ownership_type varchar [note: 'Type of ownership (e.g., lease, purchase, fleet)']

  Note: 'This association product represents the ownership relationship between a vehicle (vin_registry) and a party (customer). It captures acquisition and disposition dates, ownership type, and ownership number, tracking the history of which parties own which vehicles.. Existence Justification: A vehicle can be owned by multiple parties over its lifecycle (e.g., resale), and a party can own multiple vehicles simultaneously. The business actively records each ownership event with dates, type, and ownership number, and users query ownership history for warranty, service, recall, and resale analytics.'
}

Table manufacturing.plant {
  plant_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for each manufacturing plant or assembly facility. Primary key for the plant master record, referenced across MES, SAP PP, and logistics domains.']
  address_line1 varchar [note: 'Primary street address of the plant facility. Used for logistics, regulatory filings, and official correspondence. Classified as confidential organizational contact data.']
  address_line2 varchar [note: 'Secondary address line for the plant facility (e.g., building number, gate, or suite). Supplements address_line1 for complete facility location identification.']
  agv_enabled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant is equipped with Automated Guided Vehicle (AGV) systems for material transport on the shop floor. Used for automation capability classification and MES integration configuration.']
  annual_capacity_units varchar [note: 'Maximum number of vehicles or assemblies the plant is designed to produce per year under normal two-shift operations. Used for capacity planning, CapEx justification, and production scheduling in SAP PP.']
  city varchar [note: 'City or municipality where the plant is physically located. Used for logistics planning, workforce commute analysis, and regulatory jurisdiction determination.']
  code varchar [note: 'Externally-known alphanumeric code uniquely identifying the plant within SAP S/4HANA and MES systems (e.g., \'DETF01\', \'USNA02\'). Used as the business key across ERP, MES, and logistics integrations. Maps to SAP Organizational Unit - Plant Code.']
  commissioning_date date [note: 'The date on which the plant was officially commissioned and handed over for production operations. Distinct from SOP date; represents the physical readiness milestone. Used for asset lifecycle management and depreciation calculations in SAP AM.']
  company_code varchar [note: 'SAP S/4HANA company code to which this plant is assigned for financial accounting and controlling purposes. Links plant operations to the FI/CO module for cost center allocation and EBITDA reporting.']
  cost_center_code varchar [note: 'SAP Controlling (CO) cost center code assigned to the plant for financial cost allocation, CapEx tracking, and R&D expenditure reporting. Used in management accounting and EBITDA analysis.']
  country_code varchar [note: 'ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 three-letter country code indicating the country where the plant is physically located (e.g., \'USA\', \'DEU\', \'CHN\'). Used for regulatory compliance, CAFE reporting, and logistics routing.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the plant master record was first created in the system. Used for data lineage, audit trail, and record lifecycle management in the Databricks Silver Layer.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'ISO 4217 three-letter currency code for the local currency used in plant-level financial reporting and cost accounting (e.g., \'USD\', \'EUR\', \'CNY\'). Used in SAP FI/CO for cost center reporting and CapEx tracking.']
  daily_capacity_units varchar [note: 'Maximum number of vehicles or assemblies the plant can produce per day (Jobs Per Day - JPD). Derived from takt time and shift configuration. Used for daily production scheduling and MES throughput planning.']
  eop_date date [note: 'The official End of Production (EOP) date when the plant is scheduled to cease production of the current model or permanently close. Used for capacity planning, workforce transition, and asset disposal planning.']
  epa_facility_code varchar [note: 'EPA-assigned facility identifier for emissions and environmental compliance reporting. Required for CAFE (Corporate Average Fuel Economy) reporting and Clean Air Act compliance.']
  floor_area_sqm decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total manufacturing floor area of the plant in square meters. Used for facility planning, CapEx allocation, insurance valuation, and ISO 14001 environmental footprint reporting.']
  iatf_16949_certified boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant holds a current IATF 16949 automotive quality management system certification. Mandatory for OEM supplier qualification and customer-specific requirements compliance.']
  iso_14001_certified boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant holds a current ISO 14001 Environmental Management System certification. Required for EPA compliance reporting and sustainability disclosures.']
  iso_9001_certified boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant holds a current ISO 9001 Quality Management System certification. Required for supplier qualification and regulatory compliance reporting.']
  latitude decimal(9,6) [note: 'Geographic latitude coordinate of the plant in decimal degrees (WGS 84). Used for geospatial analytics, logistics network optimization, and supply chain mapping.']
  longitude decimal(9,6) [note: 'Geographic longitude coordinate of the plant in decimal degrees (WGS 84). Used for geospatial analytics, logistics network optimization, and supply chain mapping.']
  manager varchar [note: 'Full name of the plant manager or general manager responsible for overall plant operations. Used for escalation routing, regulatory correspondence, and organizational reporting. Not classified as PII as this is a business role reference.']
  mes_plant_code varchar [note: 'The plant identifier as registered in the Apriso/Dassault MES system. Used for cross-system reconciliation between SAP PP production orders and MES shop floor execution records. Enables traceability from ERP to shop floor.']
  model_year_current varchar [note: 'The current Model Year (MY) of vehicles being produced at the plant (e.g., \'2025\'). Used for production scheduling, regulatory compliance submissions, and CAFE reporting alignment.']
  name varchar [note: 'Official full name of the manufacturing plant or assembly facility (e.g., \'Detroit Final Assembly Plant No. 1\'). Used in reporting, regulatory submissions, and communications.']
  nhtsa_facility_code varchar [note: 'NHTSA-assigned facility identifier for the plant. Required for vehicle recall management, safety defect reporting, and Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standards (FMVSS) compliance submissions.']
  number_of_assembly_lines boolean [note: 'Total count of active production or assembly lines within the plant. Used for capacity planning, MES line configuration, and production scheduling.']
  number_of_shifts boolean [note: 'Number of production shifts operated per day at the plant (typically 1, 2, or 3). Drives capacity calculations, workforce planning in SuccessFactors, and MES scheduling.']
  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the plant. \'active\' indicates full production; \'idle\' indicates temporary suspension; \'ramp_up\' indicates pre-SOP commissioning phase; \'ramp_down\' indicates post-EOP wind-down; \'shutdown\' indicates planned or unplanned stoppage; \'decommissioned\' indicates permanently closed.']
  ota_capable boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant has the infrastructure to perform OTA (Over-the-Air) software updates on vehicles during the final assembly or PDI (Pre-Delivery Inspection) process. Relevant for connected vehicle production.']
  phone_number varchar [note: 'Primary telephone number for the plant facility. Used for operational communications, emergency contact, and supplier/logistics coordination. Classified as confidential organizational contact data.']
  plant_type varchar [note: 'Categorical classification of the plant\'s primary manufacturing function. Values include stamping (metal forming), body_shop (body-in-white welding), paint (paint application), final_assembly (vehicle assembly), ckd (Completely Knocked Down assembly), skd (Semi Knocked Down assembly), powertrain (engine/transmission), logistics (parts distribution). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: stamping|body_shop|paint|final_assembly|ckd|skd|powertrain|logistics — promote to reference product]']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code of the plant location. Used for logistics routing, tax jurisdiction assignment, and regulatory reporting.']
  powertrain_types_produced varchar [note: 'Classification of powertrain technologies assembled or manufactured at this plant. ICE (Internal Combustion Engine), EV (Electric Vehicle), HEV (Hybrid Electric Vehicle), PHEV (Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle), FCEV (Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle), or mixed. Used for EPA emissions compliance, CAFE reporting, and R&D investment tracking.']
  primary_vehicle_segment varchar [note: 'The primary vehicle segment produced at this plant. Values: car (passenger cars), truck (light/heavy trucks), suv (Sport Utility Vehicles), commercial_vehicle (vans/buses/heavy commercial), ev (Battery Electric Vehicle), hev (Hybrid Electric Vehicle - HEV), phev (Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle - PHEV), mixed (multiple segments). Drives model year planning and regulatory compliance requirements. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: car|truck|suv|commercial_vehicle|ev|hev|phev|mixed — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  region varchar [note: 'Geographic or operational region in which the plant is located (e.g., \'North America\', \'Europe\', \'Asia Pacific\'). Used for regional production planning, capacity allocation, and management reporting.']
  scada_enabled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant operates a SCADA (Supervisory Control and Data Acquisition) system for real-time monitoring and control of manufacturing processes, utilities, and equipment.']
  short_name varchar [note: 'Abbreviated or commonly used name for the plant used in dashboards, shop floor displays, and internal communications (e.g., \'Detroit FA1\').']
  sop_date date [note: 'The official Start of Production (SOP) date when the plant commenced or is scheduled to commence volume production. Critical milestone for capital planning, workforce ramp-up, and supplier readiness. Aligns with APQP phase-gate milestones.']
  state_province varchar [note: 'State, province, or administrative subdivision where the plant is located (e.g., \'Michigan\', \'Bavaria\', \'Ontario\'). Used for local regulatory compliance, tax jurisdiction, and workforce reporting.']
  takt_time_seconds varchar [note: 'The rate at which vehicles must be produced to meet customer demand, expressed in seconds per unit. Calculated as available production time divided by customer demand rate. Fundamental to JIS (Just-in-Sequence) scheduling and line balancing in MES.']
  time_zone varchar [note: 'IANA time zone identifier for the plant location (e.g., \'America/Detroit\', \'Europe/Berlin\'). Used for MES shift scheduling, production timestamp normalization, and cross-plant reporting in the Databricks lakehouse.']
  union_represented boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the plant workforce is represented by a labor union. Impacts workforce planning, shift scheduling, overtime policies, and collective bargaining agreement compliance in SuccessFactors.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the plant master record was last modified. Used for change tracking, data synchronization between SAP S/4HANA and the Databricks lakehouse, and audit compliance.']
  workforce_headcount varchar [note: 'Total number of employees (direct and indirect labor) currently employed at the plant. Used for workforce planning, capacity analysis, and regulatory headcount reporting in SuccessFactors.']

  Note: 'Master record for each manufacturing plant or assembly facility operated by Automotive. Captures plant identity, location, type (stamping, body shop, paint, final assembly, CKD/SKD), production capacity, SOP/EOP dates, plant code, and operational status. SSOT for plant-level identity referenced across MES, SAP PP, and logistics domains.'
}

Table manufacturing.production_line {
  production_line_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for each production line record within the manufacturing data platform. Serves as the primary key for the production_line entity. Role: MASTER_RESOURCE.']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant where this production line is physically located. Links to the plant master record for capacity planning, OEE reporting, and regulatory compliance reporting by facility.']
  sap_work_center_id varchar [note: 'The SAP S/4HANA PP (Production Planning) work center identifier corresponding to this production line. Enables direct linkage between the lakehouse production_line master and SAP production orders, capacity planning, and cost center assignments in SAP CO (Controlling).']
  agv_integration_enabled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this production line is integrated with an AGV (Automated Guided Vehicle) system for automated material delivery, WIP (Work in Progress) transport, or carrier movement. True = AGV-integrated; False = manual or conventional conveyor-based material handling. Used for automation investment tracking and MES configuration.']
  automation_level varchar [note: 'Classification of the overall automation level of the production line: manual (predominantly human-operated), semi_automated (mix of manual and robotic operations), highly_automated (majority robotic with human oversight), or fully_automated (lights-out or near-lights-out operation). Used for workforce planning, CapEx investment analysis, and Industry 4.0 maturity assessments.']
  ckd_skd_capable varchar [note: 'Indicates the assembly kit type this line is configured to process: CKD (Completely Knocked Down — full kit assembly from parts), SKD (Semi Knocked Down — partial pre-assembly), CBU (Completely Built Up — no kit assembly, line-off only), or multi (capable of multiple kit types). Relevant for export assembly plants and regulatory homologation.']
  conveyor_type varchar [note: 'Type of conveyor or carrier system used to move vehicles or assemblies along this production line: overhead_conveyor (hanging carrier), floor_conveyor (ground-level chain/belt), skillet (moving platform for final assembly), agv_carrier (autonomous guided vehicle carrier), stationary (fixed-position assembly), or mixed (combination of types).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this production line master record was first created in the data platform (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX). Provides audit trail for data governance, lineage tracking, and IATF 16949 record retention compliance. Maps to RECORD_AUDIT_CREATED category for MASTER_RESOURCE role.']
  current_jph decimal(10,2) [note: 'The current actual or most recently measured throughput rate of the production line in Jobs Per Hour (JPH), reflecting real-world performance after accounting for constraints, model mix, and current configuration. Used to compare against designed_jph for capacity gap analysis and production scheduling.']
  designed_jph decimal(10,2) [note: 'The engineered design throughput capacity of the production line expressed in Jobs Per Hour (JPH) — the number of vehicle units or assemblies the line is designed to produce per hour under optimal conditions. This is the nameplate capacity used for capacity planning, takt time calculation, and OEE baseline. Maps to MEASUREMENT_OR_VALUE category for MASTER_RESOURCE role.']
  energy_consumption_kwh_per_unit decimal(12,4) [note: 'Average energy consumption of this production line in kilowatt-hours (kWh) per vehicle unit produced. Used for ISO 14001 environmental management reporting, carbon footprint calculation, energy efficiency benchmarking, and EPA/CARB manufacturing emissions compliance.']
  eop_date date [note: 'The planned or actual End of Production (EOP) date for this production line — the date on which series production of the current vehicle program is scheduled to cease. Used for capacity reallocation planning, tooling disposition, and supply chain wind-down management. Nullable for lines with no defined EOP.']
  floor_space_sqm decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total floor space occupied by this production line in square meters, including the line footprint, buffer zones, and material staging areas. Used for facility capacity planning, real estate cost allocation, and plant layout optimization.']
  iatf_audit_status varchar [note: 'Current IATF 16949 (International Automotive Task Force) quality management system audit status for this production line: compliant (no non-conformances), minor_nc (minor non-conformance identified), major_nc (major non-conformance — corrective action required), suspended (production suspended pending corrective action), or not_audited (not yet subject to IATF audit).']
  jis_enabled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this production line operates under JIS (Just-in-Sequence) material delivery sequencing, where parts are delivered to the line in the exact sequence of vehicle build order. True = JIS-enabled; False = standard JIT or batch delivery. Critical for supply chain integration and sequencing system configuration.']
  last_iatf_audit_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent IATF 16949 quality management system audit conducted on this production line. Used for audit cycle management, corrective action tracking, and regulatory compliance reporting to OEM customers and certification bodies.']
  last_maintenance_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent planned preventive maintenance activity performed on this production line. Used for maintenance scheduling in SAP PM (Plant Maintenance), OEE (Overall Equipment Effectiveness) calculation, and IATF 16949 preventive maintenance compliance.']
  line_code varchar [note: 'Externally-known alphanumeric code that uniquely identifies the production line within the plant and across the enterprise (e.g., FA-01, BS-03, PT-02). Used as the business key in MES, SAP PP, and Apriso/Dassault shop-floor execution systems. Maps to BUSINESS_IDENTIFIER category for MASTER_RESOURCE role.']
  line_length_meters decimal(10,2) [note: 'Physical length of the production line in meters, measured from the first to the last station. Used for facility layout planning, AGV (Automated Guided Vehicle) path configuration, and ergonomic compliance assessments per plant engineering standards.']
  line_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable descriptive name of the production line (e.g., \'Final Assembly Line 1 – Truck Platform\', \'Body Shop Framing Line – SUV\'). Used in reporting dashboards, capacity planning, and operator interfaces. Maps to IDENTITY_LABEL category for MASTER_RESOURCE role.']
  line_status varchar [note: 'Current operational lifecycle status of the production line: active (running production), idle (scheduled downtime or no orders), maintenance (planned or unplanned maintenance), ramp_up (pre-SOP commissioning), ramp_down (approaching EOP), or decommissioned (permanently retired). Maps to LIFECYCLE_STATUS category for MASTER_RESOURCE role.']
  line_type varchar [note: 'Categorical classification of the production line by manufacturing process area: stamping (metal forming), body_shop (welding/framing), paint (surface treatment), final_assembly (vehicle build), powertrain (engine/transmission), chassis (underbody), or trim (interior/exterior finishing). Maps to CLASSIFICATION_OR_TYPE category for MASTER_RESOURCE role. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: stamping|body_shop|paint|final_assembly|powertrain|chassis|trim|subassembly|battery_pack|quality_gate — promote to reference product]']
  max_vehicle_weight_kg decimal(10,2) [note: 'Maximum gross vehicle weight in kilograms that this production line\'s conveyor, tooling, and fixtures are rated to handle. Constrains which vehicle programs can be assigned to this line and is critical for structural safety compliance and tooling certification.']
  mes_line_code varchar [note: 'The line identifier as registered in the Apriso/Dassault MES (Manufacturing Execution System) for shop floor execution, work order dispatch, and build traceability. Used for cross-system reconciliation between the lakehouse silver layer and the operational MES source system.']
  mixed_model_capable boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this production line is capable of producing multiple vehicle models or variants in a mixed sequence on the same line (mixed-model assembly). True = mixed-model capable; False = dedicated single-model line. Drives production scheduling complexity and sequencing logic in MES.']
  model_year_capability varchar [note: 'The model year (MY) or range of model years that this production line is currently tooled and certified to produce (e.g., \'2024\' or \'2024-2026\'). Drives SOP (Start of Production) and EOP (End of Production) planning, tooling investment decisions, and homologation scheduling.']
  next_maintenance_date date [note: 'Date of the next planned preventive maintenance activity scheduled for this production line. Drives maintenance work order creation in SAP PM, production scheduling adjustments, and downtime planning to minimize impact on JPH targets.']
  number_of_stations varchar [note: 'Total count of individual work stations or assembly stations on this production line. Used for line balancing analysis, ergonomic assessments, and determining the number of operators required per shift. Key input for APQP (Advanced Product Quality Planning) and control plan development.']
  paint_booth_type varchar [note: 'Type of paint application system used on this line (applicable to paint shop lines): waterborne (water-based paint — lower VOC emissions), solventborne (solvent-based paint), powder_coat (electrostatic powder coating), or none (non-paint line). Drives EPA/CARB VOC (Volatile Organic Compound) emissions compliance reporting and environmental permit management.']
  plc_system_reference varchar [note: 'Identifier or name of the PLC (Programmable Logic Controller) system or network controlling this production line\'s automated equipment (e.g., \'Siemens S7-1500 Network A\', \'Allen-Bradley ControlLogix Zone 3\'). Used for maintenance management in SAP PM, SCADA integration, and cybersecurity asset inventory.']
  powertrain_type_capability varchar [note: 'The powertrain technology types this production line is capable of assembling: ICE (Internal Combustion Engine), HEV (Hybrid Electric Vehicle), PHEV (Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle), BEV (Battery Electric Vehicle), FCEV (Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle), or multi (flexible line supporting multiple powertrain types). Critical for EV transition capacity planning and regulatory compliance with EPA/CARB emissions standards.']
  quality_gate_count varchar [note: 'Number of quality inspection gates or checkpoints integrated into this production line (e.g., in-process inspection stations, end-of-line audit points). Used for quality planning per APQP (Advanced Product Quality Planning), control plan documentation, and IATF 16949 compliance audits.']
  robot_count varchar [note: 'Total number of industrial robots (welding, painting, assembly, material handling) deployed on this production line. Used for maintenance planning in SAP PM, automation investment tracking, and Industry 4.0 KPI reporting.']
  safety_certification_expiry_date date [note: 'Expiry date of the production line\'s current safety certification. Used to trigger renewal workflows, prevent operation of uncertified lines, and support regulatory audit readiness for OSHA, DOT, and IATF 16949 compliance.']
  safety_certification_status varchar [note: 'Current status of the production line\'s safety certification and compliance with applicable occupational safety standards (e.g., OSHA, CE Machinery Directive, ISO 13849): certified (valid and current), expired (certification lapsed), pending_renewal (renewal in progress), or not_required (exempt from certification requirement).']
  scada_system_reference varchar [note: 'Identifier or name of the SCADA (Supervisory Control and Data Acquisition) system monitoring and controlling this production line (e.g., \'Ignition SCADA Plant2-Zone1\', \'Wonderware InTouch Line FA-01\'). Used for real-time production monitoring, alarm management, and integration with MES and ERP systems.']
  shift_configuration varchar [note: 'Current shift schedule configuration for the production line, indicating the number of operating shifts per day: 1_shift (single day shift), 2_shift (day and afternoon), 3_shift (continuous 24-hour), or 4_shift_rotating (rotating continental schedule). Drives capacity planning, workforce scheduling in SuccessFactors, and MRP calculations in SAP PP.']
  sop_date date [note: 'The official Start of Production (SOP) date for this production line — the date on which the line commenced or is scheduled to commence series production of the current vehicle program. Used for program milestone tracking, APQP phase gate management, and regulatory homologation scheduling.']
  takt_time_seconds decimal(10,3) [note: 'The calculated takt time for this production line in seconds — the maximum allowable time to produce one unit to meet customer demand. Derived from available production time divided by customer demand rate. Stored as a master configuration value for scheduling and line balancing in JIS (Just-in-Sequence) operations.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to this production line master record in the data platform (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX). Used for change tracking, data freshness monitoring, and incremental ETL processing in the Databricks Lakehouse silver layer.']
  vehicle_platform_code varchar [note: 'The vehicle platform or architecture code that this production line is primarily configured to build (e.g., \'F-150 Gen14\', \'GMT800\', \'MEB\'). A single line may support multiple model variants on the same platform. Used for PLM integration with Siemens Teamcenter and production scheduling.']
  weld_gun_count varchar [note: 'Total number of resistance spot weld guns (robotic and manual) deployed on this production line. Applicable primarily to body shop lines. Used for weld quality management per IATF 16949, preventive maintenance scheduling in SAP PM, and SPC (Statistical Process Control) monitoring of weld quality.']

  Note: 'Master record for each production line within a plant, including stamping lines, body shop lines, paint booths, and final assembly lines. Tracks line code, line type, designed throughput (JPH - Jobs Per Hour), current shift configuration, AGV integration flags, PLC/SCADA system references, and operational status. Owned by manufacturing as the SSOT for line-level capacity and configuration.'
}

Table manufacturing.work_center {
  work_center_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for the work center (station) record within the manufacturing data platform. Primary key for the work_center master entity.']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.plant. Business justification: Work‑center cost tracking for OEE and labor cost reporting uses a finance cost center; replacing cost_center_code with cost_center_id is standard practice.']
  agv_integration_enabled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this work center is integrated with AGV (Automated Guided Vehicle) systems for automated material delivery and WIP transport. Drives AGV routing configuration in the MES.']
  automation_level varchar [note: 'Degree of automation at this work center: fully_automated (no human intervention), highly_automated (minimal human oversight), semi_automated (human-assisted), manual (entirely human-operated), or collaborative (human-robot collaboration). Informs CapEx planning and Industry 4.0 transformation roadmap.']
  available_capacity_hours_per_day decimal(6,2) [note: 'Total available production hours per day for this work center, accounting for planned shift schedules and scheduled breaks. Used in SAP PP capacity requirements planning (CRP) and MES scheduling.']
  capacity_category varchar [note: 'Capacity classification of the work center: bottleneck (constraining throughput of the line), standard (normal capacity), buffer (excess capacity used to absorb variation), or flexible (can be redeployed across lines). Critical for Theory of Constraints analysis and production scheduling.']
  category varchar [note: 'SAP PP work center category classifying the primary resource type: machine (machine-driven capacity), labor (labor-driven capacity), machine_labor (both machine and labor capacity), or external (subcontracted operation). Determines how capacity and costing are calculated in SAP.']
  code varchar [note: 'Externally-known alphanumeric code that uniquely identifies the work center across MES, SAP PP, and SCADA systems. Used as the business key in cross-system integration (e.g., Apriso/Dassault MES work center identifier).']
  commissioning_date date [note: 'Date on which this work center was officially commissioned and made available for production use. Used for asset age tracking, depreciation calculation, and maintenance lifecycle management.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this work center master record was first created in the data platform. Used for data lineage, audit trail, and record lifecycle management.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'ISO 4217 three-letter currency code for the cost rates (machine_rate_per_hour, labor_rate_per_hour) associated with this work center (e.g., USD, EUR, JPY).']
  cycle_time_seconds decimal(10,3) [note: 'Standard cycle time in seconds for this work center to complete one unit of work. Used in production scheduling, capacity calculation, and bottleneck analysis. Represents the designed throughput rate per station.']
  decommissioning_date date [note: 'Date on which this work center was or is planned to be permanently decommissioned and removed from production service. Nullable for active stations. Used for asset lifecycle planning and capacity forecasting.']
  description varchar [note: 'Detailed textual description of the work center\'s function, scope of operations, and any special capabilities or constraints relevant to production planning and scheduling.']
  energy_consumption_kw decimal(10,2) [note: 'Rated energy consumption of this work center in kilowatts (kW) under standard operating conditions. Used for energy management, sustainability reporting, and ISO 14001 environmental compliance tracking.']
  floor_area_sqm decimal(8,2) [note: 'Physical floor area occupied by this work center in square meters. Used for facility layout planning, ergonomic assessments, and plant capacity management.']
  is_bottleneck boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this work center has been identified as a production bottleneck constraining overall line throughput. Bottleneck stations receive priority in capacity planning, maintenance scheduling, and continuous improvement initiatives.']
  is_jis_enabled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this work center participates in JIS (Just-in-Sequence) production sequencing. When true, the MES enforces strict vehicle sequence delivery to this station to support mixed-model assembly.']
  is_quality_gate boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this work center serves as a quality gate (inspection checkpoint) in the production flow. Quality gate stations require SPC (Statistical Process Control) data capture and may hold WIP pending quality approval.']
  labor_rate_per_hour decimal(10,2) [note: 'Standard direct labor cost rate per hour (in local currency) for this work center. Used in production order costing, standard cost roll-up, and labor efficiency variance reporting.']
  last_calibration_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent calibration or measurement system verification performed on this work center\'s tooling and gauges. Required for IATF 16949 Clause 7.1.5 measurement system compliance.']
  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to this work center master record. Used for change tracking, data freshness monitoring, and incremental ETL processing in the Databricks lakehouse.']
  location_description varchar [note: 'Textual description of the physical location of the work center within the plant (e.g., \'Building A, Bay 3, Row 5\'). Supports maintenance routing, safety inspections, and shop floor navigation.']
  machine_rate_per_hour decimal(10,2) [note: 'Standard machine cost rate per hour (in local currency) for this work center, used in production order costing, standard cost calculation, and variance analysis in SAP CO.']
  maintenance_strategy_code varchar [note: 'Maintenance strategy applied to this work center: preventive (scheduled interval-based), predictive (condition-monitoring-based), corrective (repair after failure), or condition_based (triggered by sensor thresholds). Drives SAP PM maintenance planning.']
  mean_time_between_failures_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Standard mean time between failures (MTBF) in hours for this work center, representing the expected average operational time between unplanned breakdowns. Used in reliability analysis, maintenance planning, and OEE (Overall Equipment Effectiveness) benchmarking.']
  mean_time_to_repair_hours decimal(8,2) [note: 'Standard mean time to repair (MTTR) in hours for this work center, representing the average time required to restore the station to operational status after a failure. Used in maintenance planning and availability calculations.']
  mes_work_center_code varchar [note: 'Native work center identifier in the Apriso/Dassault MES system. Used for cross-system traceability between the lakehouse silver layer and the operational MES for shop floor execution and WIP (Work in Progress) tracking.']
  name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name of the work center station (e.g., \'Body Framing Station 04\', \'Final Torque Assembly Line 2\'). Used in shop floor displays, MES dashboards, and production reporting.']
  next_calibration_due_date date [note: 'Scheduled date for the next calibration or measurement system verification at this work center. Triggers maintenance work orders in SAP PM and quality alerts in the MES when approaching or overdue.']
  number_of_shifts varchar [note: 'Number of production shifts per day this work center operates (typically 1, 2, or 3). Used in daily capacity calculation and workforce planning.']
  number_of_workers varchar [note: 'Standard number of direct labor workers assigned to this work center per shift. Used for workforce planning, labor cost allocation, and ergonomic assessment.']
  overhead_rate_percent decimal(6,3) [note: 'Manufacturing overhead absorption rate as a percentage applied to this work center\'s direct costs for product costing and standard cost calculation in SAP CO.']
  plc_node_code varchar [note: 'Identifier of the PLC (Programmable Logic Controller) node associated with this work center in the shop floor control network. Used for real-time production control integration and SCADA data correlation.']
  process_category varchar [note: 'Manufacturing process category to which this work center belongs, reflecting the major shop segment (e.g., stamping, body shop, paint, final assembly, powertrain). Used for shop floor segmentation, capacity analysis, and APQP (Advanced Product Quality Planning) alignment. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: stamping|body_shop|paint|final_assembly|powertrain|chassis|trim|quality_inspection|logistics — promote to reference product]']
  production_line_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the production line (e.g., stamping, body shop, paint, final assembly) to which this work center belongs. Supports JIS (Just-in-Sequence) scheduling and MES line-level reporting.']
  sap_work_center_code varchar [note: 'Native work center identifier in SAP S/4HANA PP module. Used for cross-system reconciliation between the lakehouse and SAP for production order routing, capacity planning, and cost center allocation.']
  scada_node_code varchar [note: 'Identifier of the SCADA (Supervisory Control and Data Acquisition) node associated with this work center. Enables real-time monitoring, alarm management, and production data acquisition from the shop floor.']
  setup_time_minutes decimal(8,2) [note: 'Standard setup/changeover time in minutes required to prepare this work center for a new production run or model variant. Used in SAP PP capacity planning and MES work order scheduling.']
  shift_model_code varchar [note: 'Code referencing the shift schedule model assigned to this work center (e.g., 1-shift, 2-shift, 3-shift, weekend shift). Determines available production windows for capacity planning and MES scheduling.']
  standard_value_key varchar [note: 'SAP PP standard value key (e.g., SAP1, SAP2) defining which standard values (setup, machine, labor, teardown times) are relevant for this work center in routing operations. Governs how production order times are calculated.']
  takt_time_seconds decimal(10,3) [note: 'Takt time in seconds representing the rate at which vehicles must be produced to meet customer demand. Calculated as available production time divided by customer demand rate. Used for JIS (Just-in-Sequence) line balancing and MES pacing.']
  teardown_time_minutes decimal(8,2) [note: 'Standard teardown/cleanup time in minutes required after completing a production run at this work center. Included in total station time calculations for capacity planning.']
  work_center_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the work center: active (in production use), inactive (temporarily not in use), under_maintenance (scheduled or unscheduled maintenance), decommissioned (permanently retired), or planned (not yet commissioned). Drives MES scheduling eligibility and capacity planning.']
  work_center_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the work center by its operational mode: manual (human-operated), automated (machine-driven without robotics), robotic (robot arm/AGV-integrated), semi_automated (human-assisted automation), inspection (quality gate), or rework (defect correction station). Drives capacity planning and labor allocation.']

  Note: 'Master record for individual work centers (stations) within a production line. Captures work center code, name, type (manual, automated, robotic), cycle time standard, takt time, capacity category, shift availability, and associated PLC/SCADA node identifiers. Supports JIS sequencing and MES shop floor execution at the station level.'
}

Table manufacturing.production_order {
  production_order_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for production_order']
  configuration_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the vehicle configuration or variant being built under this production order. Links to the vehicle configuration master (model, trim, powertrain, options) managed in Siemens Teamcenter PLM or SAP Variant Configuration.']
  customer_party_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to customer.party. Business justification: Order‑to‑customer linkage required for order fulfillment, warranty, and recall reports; manufacturers need to know which party placed each production order.']
  model_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.model. Business justification: Production Order release process checks Homologation_Record to ensure model/year has regulatory approval before manufacturing starts.']
  order_line_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.order_line. Business justification: In SAP PP-SD integration, a production order is created to fulfill a specific sales order line (schedule line). This link enables order confirmation, delivery date commitment, and production cost allo']
  organization_account_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to customer.organization_account. Business justification: Required for fleet sales processing: link production order to the purchasing fleet account for order tracking, invoicing, and delivery coordination.']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant where this production order is executed. Corresponds to the SAP Plant (WERKS) organizational unit. Links to the plant master data product.']
  procurement_purchase_order_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to procurement.procurement_purchase_order. Business justification: In automotive ERP (SAP PP-MM), subcontract production orders are directly linked to procurement purchase orders for cost settlement, goods issue/receipt posting, and delivery monitoring. This PP→MM in']
  procurement_supplier_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to procurement.supplier. Business justification: Enables Production Order Supplier Impact Analysis, identifying the primary supplier for critical components.']
  production_bom_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the Bill of Materials (BOM) version used for this production order. The BOM defines all components and sub-assemblies required to build the target material. Managed in SAP PP and Siemens Teamcenter PLM.']
  production_line_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific production line (e.g., stamping, body shop, paint, final assembly) assigned to execute this order. Maps to SAP Work Center (ARBPL) or MES line definition.']
  production_schedule_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.production_schedule. Business justification: Production orders are issued to fulfill a master production schedule (MPS). Many production orders are created under one production_schedule. This FK establishes the standard parent-child relationship']
  routing_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the production routing (task list / operation sequence) assigned to this order. The routing defines the sequence of manufacturing operations, work centers, and standard times. Maps to SAP PP Routing (PLNNR).']
  sku_master_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the SAP Material Master (MATNR) representing the finished vehicle, sub-assembly, or component being produced. The primary product/material being manufactured under this order.']
  actual_cost decimal(18,2) [note: 'The total actual cost incurred to execute this production order, including material consumption, labor, machine, and overhead costs as settled in SAP CO. Expressed in the plant\'s local currency. Used for cost variance analysis, profitability reporting, and CapEx/OpEx tracking.']
  actual_finish_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The precise date and time when all production activities for this order were completed and the final goods receipt was posted, as recorded by MES or SAP PP. Used for cycle time calculation, OTD (On-Time Delivery) measurement, and throughput analysis.']
  actual_labor_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'The total actual direct labor hours consumed during execution of this production order, as confirmed through SAP PP operation confirmations or MES time tracking. Used for labor efficiency analysis, variance reporting, and cost actuals in SAP CO.']
  actual_machine_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'The total actual machine/equipment hours consumed during execution of this production order, as recorded by SCADA (Supervisory Control and Data Acquisition) systems or MES. Used for OEE analysis, machine utilization reporting, and cost variance calculation.']
  actual_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The precise date and time when production activities for this order actually commenced on the shop floor, as recorded by the MES or confirmed by the first operation confirmation in SAP PP. Used for cycle time analysis and schedule adherence measurement.']
  assembly_sequence_number varchar [note: 'The Just-in-Sequence (JIS) sequence number assigned to this production order, defining its position in the assembly line build sequence. Critical for JIS supply chain synchronization — ensures parts arrive at the line in the exact order vehicles are built. Managed by MES and communicated to tier-1 suppliers.']
  confirmed_quantity decimal(15,3) [note: 'The quantity of vehicles or sub-assemblies that have been confirmed as successfully produced and goods-receipted against this order. Maps to SAP PP confirmed yield quantity (WEMNG). Used for production performance and yield tracking.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when this production order record was first created in the source system (SAP PP). Used for audit trail, data lineage, and record lifecycle management. Maps to SAP order creation timestamp (ERDAT + ERZET).']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'ISO 4217 three-letter currency code for the standard_cost and actual_cost amounts on this production order (e.g., USD, EUR, JPY, MXN). Determined by the plant\'s controlling area currency in SAP CO.']
  hazardous_material_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this production order involves the use or production of hazardous materials (e.g., battery electrolytes for EV/HEV powertrains, paint solvents, chemical treatments). True = hazardous materials involved, requiring compliance with EPA, CARB, and ISO 14001 environmental management protocols.']
  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when this production order record was most recently modified in the source system. Used for change tracking, delta load processing in the lakehouse pipeline, and audit compliance under IATF 16949.']
  mes_order_reference varchar [note: 'Cross-reference identifier assigned by the Apriso/Dassault MES system for this production order. Used to correlate SAP PP orders with shop floor execution records in the MES for traceability and real-time production control.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'The vehicle model year (MY) associated with the units being produced under this order. Critical for regulatory compliance (CAFE, EPA, NCAP), warranty tracking, and production program management. Typically a 4-digit year (e.g., 2025).']
  order_date date [note: 'The business date on which the production order was created and issued in SAP PP. This is the principal business event date representing when the manufacturing work order was formally raised. Maps to SAP order creation date (ERDAT).']
  order_description varchar [note: 'Free-text description of the production order providing additional context about the manufacturing scope, special instructions, or engineering change notes. Used by shop floor supervisors and production planners for operational clarity.']
  order_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known alphanumeric identifier for the production order as assigned by SAP PP (Production Planning) or Apriso MES. This is the business-facing reference used on the shop floor, in MES screens, and in cross-system communication. Maps to SAP PP order number (AUFNR).']
  order_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the production order within the SAP PP and MES workflow. \'created\' = order created but not yet released to shop floor; \'released\' = released for execution; \'in_progress\' = active manufacturing underway; \'partially_confirmed\' = some operations confirmed; \'confirmed\' = all operations confirmed; \'technically_completed\' = TECO status in SAP — production complete, pending settlement; \'closed\' = fully settled and archived; \'cancelled\' = order voided. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: created|released|in_progress|partially_confirmed|confirmed|technically_completed|closed|cancelled — promote to reference product]']
  order_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the production order indicating its manufacturing purpose. \'vehicle_build\' represents a full vehicle assembly order; \'sub_assembly\' covers component or module builds (e.g., powertrain, axle); \'rework\' covers defect correction orders; \'repair\' covers warranty or field return repairs; \'prototype\' covers R&D and pre-SOP (Start of Production) builds. Maps to SAP PP order type (AUART).']
  planned_finish_date date [note: 'The scheduled date on which all production activities for this order are planned to be completed. Used for delivery commitment, JIT (Just-in-Time) scheduling, and OTD (On-Time Delivery) performance tracking. Maps to SAP PP planned finish date (GLTRS).']
  planned_labor_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'The total planned direct labor hours required to execute this production order, derived from the routing standard times and target quantity. Used for capacity planning, workforce scheduling via SuccessFactors, and standard cost calculation in SAP CO.']
  planned_machine_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'The total planned machine/equipment hours required to execute this production order, derived from routing standard machine times. Used for equipment capacity planning, OEE (Overall Equipment Effectiveness) baseline, and standard cost calculation.']
  planned_start_date date [note: 'The scheduled date on which production activities for this order are planned to begin, as determined by MRP (Material Requirements Planning) or production scheduling. Maps to SAP PP planned start date (GSTRS).']
  ppap_required boolean [note: 'Indicates whether PPAP (Production Part Approval Process) approval is required before this production order can be released for full-volume production. Typically True for new part introductions, engineering changes, or supplier changes. Governed by IATF 16949 and AIAG PPAP standards.']
  priority varchar [note: 'Numeric priority level assigned to this production order for shop floor scheduling and sequencing. Lower values indicate higher priority. Used by MES and JIS (Just-in-Sequence) systems to determine execution order when multiple orders compete for the same resources.']
  production_stage varchar [note: 'The manufacturing stage or shop area where this production order is executed. Reflects the physical production process: \'stamping\' = metal forming; \'body_shop\' = body-in-white welding; \'paint\' = surface coating; \'final_assembly\' = vehicle assembly; \'powertrain\' = engine/transmission build; \'trim_chassis\' = trim, chassis, and final fit.']
  quality_inspection_lot varchar [note: 'The SAP QM (Quality Management) inspection lot number created for quality inspection activities associated with this production order. Links production execution to quality results, usage decisions, and non-conformance records. Maps to SAP QM inspection lot (QMNUM).']
  release_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when the production order was formally released to the shop floor for execution. Release triggers material staging, capacity reservation, and MES order activation. Maps to SAP PP order release date/time.']
  rework_quantity decimal(15,3) [note: 'The quantity of units that required rework (non-conforming units corrected and returned to production flow) during execution of this order. Distinct from scrap — reworked units are salvaged. Used for quality cost analysis and APQP (Advanced Product Quality Planning) reporting.']
  scrap_quantity decimal(15,3) [note: 'The quantity of units scrapped or rejected during production of this order due to quality defects or process failures. Used for PPM (Parts Per Million) defect rate calculation, scrap cost analysis, and FMEA (Failure Mode and Effects Analysis) reporting.']
  sop_indicator boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this production order is part of the SOP (Start of Production) launch phase for a new vehicle program. True = SOP launch order subject to enhanced quality monitoring and APQP controls; False = regular series production order.']
  standard_cost decimal(18,2) [note: 'The planned standard cost to produce the target quantity under this order, calculated from the BOM (Bill of Materials) component costs and routing activity rates in SAP CO (Controlling). Expressed in the plant\'s local currency. Used for cost variance analysis and EBITDA reporting.']
  target_quantity decimal(15,3) [note: 'The planned quantity of vehicles or sub-assemblies to be produced under this order, expressed in the base unit of measure (typically \'EA\' for each). Maps to SAP PP planned order quantity (GAMNG). Used for capacity planning and production scheduling.']
  technical_completion_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when the production order was set to Technically Completed (TECO) status in SAP PP, indicating all manufacturing operations are finished and the order is pending cost settlement. Distinct from actual_finish_timestamp which records the last physical operation.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'The base unit of measure for the target and confirmed quantities on this production order (e.g., EA = Each for vehicles, PC = Piece for components, SET for kits, KG for bulk materials). Maps to SAP base unit of measure (MEINS).']
  vin_range_end varchar [note: 'The last VIN (Vehicle Identification Number) in the sequential range assigned to units produced under this order. Together with vin_range_start, defines the complete set of vehicles traceable to this production order for recall, warranty, and homologation purposes.']
  vin_range_start varchar [note: 'The first VIN (Vehicle Identification Number) in the sequential range assigned to units produced under this order. VINs are 17-character alphanumeric identifiers conforming to ISO 3779. Used for build traceability, recall management, and regulatory reporting to NHTSA.']
  wip_posting_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether WIP (Work in Progress) costs have been calculated and posted to the financial ledger for this production order in the current period. True = WIP posted; False = WIP not yet posted. Used for period-end financial closing in SAP CO.']

  Note: 'Core transactional record representing a manufacturing work order issued to produce a specific vehicle or sub-assembly. Captures order number, order type (vehicle build, sub-assembly, rework), planned and actual start/finish dates, target quantity, produced quantity, order status, priority, shift assignment, and MES order reference. Links to plant, production line, and vehicle configuration. SSOT for shop floor execution tracking in SAP PP and Apriso MES.'
}

Table manufacturing.build_sequence {
  build_sequence_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for the Just-in-Sequence (JIS) build sequence record in the Databricks Silver Layer. Primary key generated by the Apriso/Dassault MES sequencing module upon sequence creation.']
  dealership_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to dealer.dealership. Business justification: Build sequences are created for specific dealer-ordered vehicles; the JIS call-off and sequence freeze process requires knowing the destination dealership. `dealer_code` is a denormalized plain attrib']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant where this build sequence is executed. Links to the plant master record in the enterprise data model.']
  primary_build_configuration_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the vehicle configuration record defining the model, trim, powertrain, and option codes for the vehicle to be assembled at this sequence position.']
  production_line_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific assembly or production line to which this build sequence is assigned. Drives AGV routing and JIS supplier call-off logic.']
  production_order_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.production_order. Business justification: Rename work_order_id to production_order_id to follow FK naming convention and link each build sequence to its parent production order.']
  production_schedule_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.production_schedule. Business justification: A JIS build sequence is derived from and governed by the master production schedule. The build_sequence defines the ordered vehicle sequence within a scheduled production window. This FK links each se']
  vehicle_order_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.vehicle_order. Business justification: In automotive MES/SAP AOM integration, a build sequence entry is created in direct response to a confirmed vehicle order — the sequence IS the manufacturing commitment to that order. Production planne']
  vin_registry_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.vin_registry. Business justification: Build sequencing records must be traceable to the VIN registry for production audit trails, homologation market assignment, and recall scope determination. The plain vin column on build_sequence is a ']
  actual_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual date and time at which assembly of the vehicle at this sequence position was completed on the production line, as recorded by the MES. Null until assembly is complete.']
  actual_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual date and time at which assembly of the vehicle at this sequence position commenced on the production line, as recorded by the MES. Null until assembly begins.']
  assembly_stage varchar [note: 'Current or target assembly stage for this sequence position: \'stamping\' = metal forming; \'body_shop\' = body-in-white welding; \'paint\' = paint application; \'final_assembly\' = trim and chassis assembly; \'PDI\' = Pre-Delivery Inspection. Drives shop floor routing in the MES.']
  body_style_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the body style of the vehicle (e.g., sedan, SUV, truck, coupe, van). Influences stamping and body shop routing within the production sequence.']
  build_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the vehicle build purpose: \'retail\' = customer-ordered retail unit; \'fleet\' = fleet customer order; \'stock\' = dealer stock build; \'pilot\' = pre-production pilot build; \'homologation\' = regulatory homologation unit; \'press\' = media/press fleet unit.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time at which this build sequence record was first created in the MES sequencing module and ingested into the Databricks Silver Layer. Supports audit trail and data lineage.']
  destination_market_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country code indicating the intended destination market for the vehicle. Determines homologation variant, regulatory compliance requirements (NHTSA, UNECE, CARB), and market-specific option content.']
  drive_configuration varchar [note: 'Drivetrain configuration of the vehicle: FWD (Front-Wheel Drive), RWD (Rear-Wheel Drive), AWD (All-Wheel Drive), or 4WD (Four-Wheel Drive). Determines axle and driveshaft JIS call-off and assembly routing.']
  exterior_color_code varchar [note: 'Paint color code for the vehicle exterior as specified in the build sequence. Drives paint shop scheduling, batch sequencing for color changeover optimization, and JIS paint material call-off.']
  freeze_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time at which the build sequence was frozen and locked for JIS supplier call-off. After this timestamp, sequence changes are restricted and require formal change control. Critical for JIS supplier synchronization.']
  interior_color_code varchar [note: 'Interior trim and upholstery color code for the vehicle. Used for JIS seat and interior component call-off to tier-1 suppliers.']
  is_frozen boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the build sequence record has been frozen for JIS supplier call-off. When True, sequence changes are restricted and require formal change control approval. Directly linked to freeze_timestamp.']
  is_priority_build boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this vehicle has been flagged as a priority build (e.g., executive order, critical dealer allocation, regulatory compliance unit). Priority builds may override standard JIS sequencing rules.']
  jis_call_off_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time at which the JIS supplier call-off message was transmitted for this sequence position. Triggers sequenced part delivery from tier-1 JIS suppliers. Critical for supply chain synchronization and OTD measurement.']
  mes_sequence_reference varchar [note: 'Native sequence record identifier from the Apriso/Dassault MES sequencing module. Serves as the source system key for lineage and reconciliation between the Silver Layer and the operational MES.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'Four-digit model year (MY) of the vehicle to be assembled at this sequence position. Determines applicable BOM revision, regulatory homologation requirements, and CAFE/EPA compliance targets.']
  option_package_code varchar [note: 'Code representing the bundled option package (e.g., technology package, towing package, safety package) selected for the vehicle. Determines additional BOM components and JIS call-off requirements.']
  planned_build_date date [note: 'Calendar date on which the vehicle at this sequence position is planned to be assembled. Used for JIS supplier call-off scheduling and production planning in SAP PP.']
  planned_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Precise date and time at which assembly of the vehicle at this sequence position is planned to complete. Used for takt time validation and shift throughput planning.']
  planned_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Precise date and time at which assembly of the vehicle at this sequence position is planned to begin on the production line. Drives JIS call-off timing and AGV dispatch scheduling.']
  powertrain_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the vehicle powertrain to be assembled: ICE (Internal Combustion Engine), HEV (Hybrid Electric Vehicle), PHEV (Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle), BEV (Battery Electric Vehicle), or FCEV (Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle). Drives line routing, tooling selection, and regulatory compliance checks.']
  quality_hold_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this build sequence position has been placed on a quality hold, preventing progression to the next assembly stage. Triggered by MES quality gate failures or FMEA-driven stop conditions.']
  regulatory_variant_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the regulatory homologation variant of the vehicle (e.g., US-NHTSA, EU-UNECE, CARB-ZEV). Ensures correct BOM variant selection for emissions, safety, and market-specific compliance requirements.']
  sequence_change_reason varchar [note: 'Free-text or coded reason for the most recent modification to this build sequence record (e.g., material shortage, quality hold, customer order change, line disruption). Required for change control audit trail under IATF 16949.']
  sequence_number varchar [note: 'Ordered position number of this vehicle within the production run for the assigned line and shift. Drives JIS supplier call-off sequencing and AGV routing. Must be unique within a plant, line, shift, and planned build date combination.']
  sequence_revision_number varchar [note: 'Incremental revision counter tracking how many times this build sequence record has been modified since initial creation. Supports audit trail and change control compliance under IATF 16949.']
  sequence_set_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known identifier for the production sequence set or run to which this record belongs, as generated by the MES sequencing module. Used for JIS call-off batch identification and supplier communication.']
  sequence_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the build sequence record within the MES workflow. \'planned\' = sequence created but not yet released to shop floor; \'released\' = released for production; \'in_progress\' = vehicle currently being assembled; \'completed\' = assembly finished; \'cancelled\' = sequence removed from run; \'on_hold\' = temporarily suspended pending resolution.']
  sequence_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the build sequence record indicating whether it is the original planned sequence, a revised sequence after change control, an emergency insertion, or a resequenced position due to line disruption.']
  takt_time_seconds varchar [note: 'Planned takt time in seconds allocated for assembly of the vehicle at this sequence position. Derived from the line rate and used for production pacing, WIP tracking, and OTD performance measurement.']
  transmission_type varchar [note: 'Type of transmission to be installed in the vehicle at this sequence position. Drives drivetrain component JIS call-off and assembly station routing.']
  trim_level_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the trim or grade level of the vehicle (e.g., base, mid, premium, sport). Determines option content, BOM variant, and JIS part call-off requirements.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time of the most recent modification to this build sequence record, as recorded in the MES or Silver Layer. Used for incremental data loading, change detection, and audit compliance.']
  vehicle_model_code varchar [note: 'Alphanumeric code identifying the vehicle model (e.g., platform or nameplate code) to be assembled at this sequence position. Sourced from Teamcenter PLM product structure.']

  Note: 'JIS (Just-in-Sequence) build sequence record defining the ordered sequence of vehicles to be assembled on a production line for a given shift or production run. Captures sequence number, planned build date, shift, line assignment, vehicle configuration reference (model, trim, powertrain, color), sequence status, and freeze timestamp. Critical for JIS supplier call-offs and AGV routing. Sourced from Apriso MES sequencing module.'
}

This FK normalizes the relationship between the actual build event and its planned sequence entry. build_sequence_number (']
  build_spec_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.build_spec. Business justification: End-of-line quality verification requires comparing the actual vehicle_build execution record against the ordered build_spec (options, safety features, regulatory approvals). This link drives build co']
  configuration_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.configuration. Business justification: Manufacturing traceability requires knowing the exact vehicle configuration (trim, options, color, powertrain) built for each VIN. End-of-line quality gates, warranty registration, and regulatory comp']
  model_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.model. Business justification: Each built vehicle must be tied to its Homologation_Record for VIN‑level compliance verification and reporting.']

  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant where this vehicle was assembled. Enables plant-level production reporting and capacity analysis.']
  powertrain_variant_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.powertrain_variant. Business justification: Recall management, warranty claims, and emissions compliance require tracing which specific powertrain variant (engine/motor/transmission combination) was assembled into each VIN. vehicle_build tracks']
  production_line_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.build_stage. Business justification: Vehicle build references its current build stage; replace string column with FK to build_stage for normalization.']
  vehicle_order_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.vehicle_order. Business justification: Enables Build ↔ Delivery scheduling; associates each built vehicle with the sales order it fulfills for handover and logistics planning.']
  vin_registry_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.vin_registry. Business justification: VIN Registration Integration needed for warranty, recall, and regulatory reporting linking build record to VIN registry.']
  assembly_line_code varchar [note: 'Identifier for the specific production/assembly line within the plant on which this vehicle was built (e.g., LINE-A1, TRIM-LINE-3). Used for line-level throughput analysis, bottleneck identification, and SCADA/PLC integration.']
  battery_serial_number varchar [note: 'The unique serial number of the high-voltage traction battery pack installed in this vehicle (applicable to HEV, PHEV, BEV). Null for ICE vehicles. Critical for EV battery genealogy, warranty, and end-of-life regulatory compliance.']
  body_shop_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the body shop stage was completed and the body-in-white was released to the paint shop. Null if stage not yet completed.']
