  body_style varchar [note: 'The body style classification of the vehicle being built. Used for production line routing, tooling selection, and regulatory homologation grouping.']
  bom_version varchar [note: 'The specific BOM (Bill of Materials) version or engineering change level used to build this vehicle. Captured at build time for genealogy and recall traceability. Links to Siemens Teamcenter PLM BOM management.']
  build_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual date and time when the vehicle build was completed at the final assembly stage and signed off for PDI (Pre-Delivery Inspection). Null if the build is still in progress. Used for cycle time, throughput, and SOP/EOP milestone tracking.']
  build_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual date and time when the vehicle build process commenced at the first production stage (typically body-in-white or stamping entry). Principal business event timestamp for the build lifecycle. Used for cycle time calculation and OTD (On-Time Delivery) analysis.']
  build_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the vehicle build record. Drives shop floor workflow, MES gating, and production reporting. \'on_hold\' indicates a quality or material hold; \'rework\' indicates a defect requiring corrective action before completion.']
  build_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the vehicle build type: standard (full local assembly), CKD (Completely Knocked Down – assembled from imported kits), SKD (Semi Knocked Down), pilot (pre-SOP trial build), or prototype (engineering validation). Affects BOM, duty classification, and regulatory reporting.']
  chassis_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the chassis assembly stage was completed. Null if stage not yet completed.']
  chassis_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the vehicle entered the chassis assembly stage for powertrain marriage, suspension, and drivetrain installation.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this vehicle build record was first created in the MES/data platform. Represents the audit trail creation event, distinct from the build_start_timestamp which is the actual shop floor event time.']
  end_of_line_test_result varchar [note: 'Result of the automated end-of-line (EOL) functional test performed on the completed vehicle, covering powertrain, brakes, ADAS, and electrical systems. Distinct from quality_gate_status which covers visual/dimensional inspection.']
  engine_serial_number varchar [note: 'The unique serial number of the engine unit installed in this vehicle during chassis assembly. Critical for powertrain genealogy, warranty traceability, and recall management.']
  final_assembly_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the final assembly stage was completed and the vehicle was released for PDI (Pre-Delivery Inspection). Null if stage not yet completed.']
  final_assembly_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the vehicle entered the final assembly stage for fluids, wheels, glass, and end-of-line checks.']
  hold_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this vehicle build is currently under a production hold (True = on hold). A hold may be triggered by a quality non-conformance, missing parts, engineering change, or safety concern. Drives MES gating to prevent further assembly until resolved.']
  hold_reason_code varchar [note: 'Coded reason for the production hold applied to this vehicle build (e.g., QUALITY_DEFECT, MISSING_PART, ECN_CHANGE, SAFETY_CONCERN). Null if hold_flag is False. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: QUALITY_DEFECT|MISSING_PART|ECN_CHANGE|SAFETY_CONCERN|SUPPLIER_DEVIATION|TOOLING_FAILURE|OTHER — promote to reference product]']
  homologation_market varchar [note: 'The target regulatory market for which this vehicle is homologated and built (e.g., USA, DEU, GBR, CHN, AUS). Determines applicable safety, emissions, and labeling standards. Uses ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country codes.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'The automotive model year (MY) designation for the vehicle being built (e.g., 2025). Determines applicable regulatory standards, BOM revision, and homologation requirements. Distinct from the calendar year of production.']
  paint_color_code varchar [note: 'The manufacturer\'s paint color code applied to this vehicle during the paint stage (e.g., G1W – Super White, 040 – Classic White). Used for paint shop scheduling, color sequencing optimization, and customer order matching.']
  paint_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the paint stage was completed and the vehicle body was released to the trim/general assembly line. Null if stage not yet completed.']
  paint_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the vehicle body entered the paint shop for priming, painting, and clear-coat application.']
  powertrain_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the vehicle powertrain technology being assembled: ICE (Internal Combustion Engine), HEV (Hybrid Electric Vehicle), PHEV (Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle), BEV (Battery Electric Vehicle), or FCEV (Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle). Drives assembly line routing, tooling, and EPA/CARB emissions compliance tracking.']
  quality_gate_status varchar [note: 'Result of the end-of-line quality gate inspection for this vehicle build. \'conditional_pass\' indicates the vehicle passed with minor deviations requiring dealer rectification. Drives release-to-shipping authorization.']
  rework_count varchar [note: 'Number of rework operations performed on this vehicle build. A count greater than zero indicates quality issues encountered during assembly. Used for PPM (Parts Per Million) defect rate calculation and FMEA analysis.']
  rework_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this vehicle build has been flagged for rework due to a detected defect or non-conformance (True = rework required). Distinct from hold_flag; a vehicle can be in rework while not on hold.']
  scheduled_build_date date [note: 'The planned calendar date on which this vehicle was scheduled to be built per the production plan (MRP/MES schedule). Used to measure schedule adherence and production plan vs. actual variance.']
  sop_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this vehicle build is part of the official Start of Production (SOP) launch batch (True = SOP vehicle). SOP vehicles are subject to enhanced quality scrutiny and PPAP sign-off requirements.']
  stamping_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the stamping stage was completed and the body panels were released to the body shop. Null if stage not yet completed.']
  stamping_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the vehicle body panels entered the stamping stage. Used for stage-level cycle time analysis and production flow monitoring.']
  transmission_serial_number varchar [note: 'The unique serial number of the transmission unit installed in this vehicle. Used for drivetrain genealogy, warranty claims, and component-level recall traceability.']
  trim_end_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the trim assembly stage was completed. Null if stage not yet completed.']
  trim_start_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Actual timestamp when the vehicle entered the trim assembly stage for interior fitting, electrical harness installation, and dashboard assembly.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to this vehicle build record. Used for incremental data pipeline processing, audit trails, and detecting stale records in the Silver layer.']
  vehicle_model_code varchar [note: 'Internal product/model code identifying the vehicle nameplate and variant being built (e.g., F150-XLT-4WD). Maps to the PLM product structure in Siemens Teamcenter. Used for BOM resolution and build sequence planning.']
  vin varchar [note: 'The globally unique 17-character Vehicle Identification Number (VIN) assigned to this vehicle unit at body-in-white or VIN stamping stage. Conforms to ISO 3779 / NHTSA VIN standard. SSOT for vehicle-level traceability across manufacturing, sales, and after-sales domains.']

  Note: 'Transactional record capturing the actual build event for a single vehicle unit on the shop floor, from body-in-white through final assembly. Tracks VIN assignment, production order reference, actual start and completion timestamps per build stage (stamping, body shop, paint, trim, chassis, final), build status, shift, operator team, and any hold or rework flags. SSOT for vehicle-level build traceability and genealogy in the manufacturing domain.'
}

Table manufacturing.production_schedule {
  production_schedule_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for the master production schedule (MPS) record in the Databricks Silver Layer. Primary key for this entity.']
  fleet_contract_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.fleet_contract. Business justification: Fleet contracts specify committed_volume and delivery_schedule_type that directly drive production schedule creation. Production planners must reference the fleet contract to validate planned_quantity']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant where the scheduled production will occur. Corresponds to the SAP Plant organizational unit.']
  production_fleet_account_organization_account_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to customer.customer_fleet_account. Business justification: Fleet customers (rental companies, government fleets) place large committed orders that directly drive production scheduling. OEM fleet program scheduling reports require linking a production schedule']
  production_line_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific production line (e.g., final assembly, body shop, paint shop, stamping) within the plant where the scheduled build will take place.']
  production_organization_account_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to customer.organization_account. Business justification: Corporate/government fleet programs are managed at the organization account level. Dedicated production runs for B2B fleet contracts (e.g., a government tender for 500 police vehicles) require linking']
  vehicle_model_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the vehicle model (e.g., sedan, SUV, truck, EV) being scheduled for production. Links to the vehicle master product record.']
  actual_quantity varchar [note: 'Actual number of vehicles produced and confirmed as good units by the MES (Manufacturing Execution System) during the schedule period. Populated upon schedule completion from Apriso/Dassault MES.']
  approved_by varchar [note: 'Name or employee identifier of the production planning manager who formally approved this schedule version for release to the shop floor. Required for IATF 16949 document control and audit trail.']
  approved_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the production schedule was formally approved for release. Marks the transition from \'draft\' to \'released\' status and triggers supplier call-off generation.']
  assembly_process_stage varchar [note: 'Manufacturing process stage covered by this schedule record. Reflects the major shop areas in automotive assembly: stamping (metal forming), body shop (welding/joining), paint (surface treatment), final assembly (powertrain/trim installation), or trim-chassis-final (TCF integrated line).']
  available_capacity_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Total available production capacity in hours for this schedule period on the assigned production line, accounting for planned downtime, maintenance windows, and shift patterns.']
  body_style varchar [note: 'Body style classification of the vehicles scheduled for production. Used for production line configuration, tooling setup, and capacity planning by body type.']
  build_sequence_end varchar [note: 'Ending sequence number for the JIS (Just-in-Sequence) build order within this schedule period. Together with build_sequence_start defines the range of vehicle sequence numbers assigned to this schedule.']
  build_sequence_start varchar [note: 'Starting sequence number for the JIS (Just-in-Sequence) build order within this schedule period. Used by MES and AGV systems to sequence vehicle bodies through the production line in the correct order.']
  build_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the production build type. \'standard\' = full series production; \'ckd\' = Completely Knocked Down (CKD) kit assembly; \'skd\' = Semi Knocked Down (SKD) assembly; \'pilot\' = pre-production pilot build; \'pre_series\' = pre-series validation build.']
  calloff_transmission_time timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when supplier delivery call-offs were transmitted to the supply base for this production schedule. Used for OTD (On-Time Delivery) tracking and supplier performance measurement.']
  capacity_utilization_pct decimal(5,2) [note: 'Planned capacity utilization percentage for this schedule period, calculated as (confirmed_quantity × takt_time) / available_capacity_hours × 100. Stored as a planning input from SAP PP capacity leveling, not a derived KPI.']
  confirmed_quantity varchar [note: 'Number of vehicles confirmed for production after capacity leveling, constraint resolution, and production planning approval. May differ from planned_quantity due to capacity constraints or material availability.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this production schedule record was first created in the system, either by an MRP run or manual entry. Audit trail field for data lineage and compliance.']
  eop_date date [note: 'Official End of Production (EOP) date for the vehicle model/model year. Schedules beyond this date are invalid. Used for phase-out planning, last-time-buy procurement, and regulatory deregistration.']
  fiscal_period varchar [note: 'Fiscal period (month number 1-12 or 1-16 for special periods) within the fiscal year. Enables alignment of production volumes with financial reporting periods for management accounting and EBITDA analysis.']
  fiscal_year varchar [note: 'Fiscal year (FY) to which this production schedule belongs. Used for financial planning, CapEx allocation, and EBITDA reporting alignment between production volumes and financial periods.']
  freeze_horizon_date date [note: 'Date up to which the production schedule is frozen and cannot be changed without formal change control. Protects supplier call-offs and JIT/JIS delivery commitments within the frozen window.']
  is_frozen boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether this schedule record is currently within the freeze horizon and locked against changes. True = frozen (no modifications allowed without change control); False = open for revision.']
  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent modification to this production schedule record. Updated on every revision, status change, or quantity adjustment. Essential for incremental data loading in the Databricks Silver Layer.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'The automotive model year (MY) designation for the vehicles being scheduled. Used for regulatory compliance (CAFE, EPA), BOM versioning, and sales planning. Typically one year ahead of the calendar year for SOP planning.']
  planned_quantity varchar [note: 'Total number of vehicles planned for production in this schedule period as determined by the MRP run. Represents the gross requirement before capacity and constraint adjustments.']
  powertrain_type varchar [note: 'Powertrain technology classification for the vehicles in this schedule. ICE = Internal Combustion Engine; HEV = Hybrid Electric Vehicle; PHEV = Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle; EV = Battery Electric Vehicle; FCEV = Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle. Drives regulatory reporting (CAFE, EPA, CARB) and BOM variant selection.']
  regulatory_market varchar [note: 'ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country code for the primary regulatory market for which these vehicles are being produced. Determines applicable homologation standards (NHTSA/FMVSS for USA, UNECE for Europe, etc.), emissions certification (EPA, CARB, Euro 6), and CAFE compliance tracking. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: USA|CAN|MEX|DEU|GBR|CHN|JPN|KOR|AUS|BRA — 10 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  schedule_change_reason varchar [note: 'Business reason code or description for the most recent schedule revision. Examples: material shortage, capacity constraint, demand change, quality hold, tooling failure, model mix adjustment. Required for change control audit trail per IATF 16949.']
  schedule_date date [note: 'The principal planned production date (or start date of the weekly/monthly bucket) for this schedule record. Represents the real-world business event date when production is planned to occur.']
  schedule_end_date date [note: 'End date of the production schedule period. For daily schedules this equals schedule_date; for weekly/monthly buckets this marks the last day of the planning period.']
  schedule_notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for production planners to record contextual notes, exceptions, or special instructions associated with this schedule record. Examples: tooling changeover notes, quality hold references, special customer order constraints.']
  schedule_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known alphanumeric identifier for the production schedule record, sourced from SAP PP (Production Planning). Used for cross-system reference and communication with supply chain and manufacturing teams.']
  schedule_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the production schedule record. \'draft\' = initial planning; \'released\' = approved for execution; \'frozen\' = within freeze horizon, no changes allowed; \'in_execution\' = active shop floor production; \'completed\' = all planned units built; \'cancelled\' = schedule voided.']
  schedule_type varchar [note: 'Time granularity of the production schedule record. \'daily\' = day-level MPS; \'weekly\' = weekly bucket plan; \'monthly\' = monthly capacity plan. Determines the planning horizon and supplier call-off frequency.']
  schedule_version varchar [note: 'Incremental version number of the production schedule. Each MRP (Material Requirements Planning) run or manual revision increments this counter, enabling version comparison and rollback analysis.']
  scheduled_end_time timestamp [note: 'Precise timestamp when production is scheduled to complete for this schedule record. Used for shift planning, takt time validation, and downstream logistics coordination.']
  scheduled_start_time timestamp [note: 'Precise timestamp when production is scheduled to begin on the line for this schedule record. Used for JIS (Just-in-Sequence) sequencing, AGV routing, and supplier call-off timing.']
  shift_pattern varchar [note: 'Shift configuration planned for this production schedule period. Determines available production hours and workforce requirements. Single = 1 shift/day; Double = 2 shifts/day; Triple = 3 shifts/day; Weekend = weekend-only production.']
  sop_date date [note: 'Official Start of Production (SOP) date for the vehicle model/model year being scheduled. Marks the formal launch of series production. Used to validate that schedule dates fall within the active production window.']
  supplier_calloff_generated boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether supplier delivery call-offs (JIT/JIS delivery schedules) have been generated and transmitted to suppliers based on this production schedule. True = call-offs sent; False = pending call-off generation.']
  takt_time_seconds varchar [note: 'Planned takt time in seconds per vehicle unit for this production schedule. Takt time = available production time divided by customer demand rate. Drives line balancing, AGV pacing, and JIS sequencing intervals.']

  Note: 'Master production schedule (MPS) record defining planned vehicle production volumes by plant, production line, model, MY (Model Year), and time period (daily, weekly, monthly). Captures scheduled quantity, confirmed quantity, schedule version, freeze horizon, schedule status, and MRP run reference. Integrates with SAP PP MRP and supports capacity planning and supplier call-off generation.'
}

Table manufacturing.production_bom {
  production_bom_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for the manufacturing Bill of Materials (mBOM) record in the Databricks Silver Layer. Serves as the primary key for this entity.']
  configuration_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.configuration. Business justification: Configuration-specific BOMs are standard in automotive (EV vs ICE variant of same model have different BOMs). Linking production_bom to configuration enables PPAP documentation, configuration-level co']
  model_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.model. Business justification: BOM Management Report requires each BOM to be tied to a vehicle model to ensure correct component list per model.']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.plant. Business justification: production_bom has a plant_code STRING field — a denormalized reference to the plant where this BOM is used. Replacing it with a proper FK to plant normalizes the relationship. plant_code is removed a']
  platform_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.platform. Business justification: Production BOMs in automotive are structured at platform level (e.g., MQB, TNGA). Platform-level BOM management drives component commonality reporting, platform cost analysis, and engineering change c']
  powertrain_variant_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.powertrain_variant. Business justification: Powertrain-variant-specific BOMs are a core automotive engineering practice — EV, PHEV, and ICE variants of the same model have distinct BOMs. Linking enables powertrain cost analysis, supplier sourci']
  procurement_supplier_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to procurement.procurement_supplier. Business justification: Automotive BOMs are single- or dual-sourced; linking BOM to nominated supplier enables PPAP validation, supplier calloff scheduling, and IATF 16949 traceability audits. Manufacturing engineers expect ']
  production_line_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.production_line. Business justification: production_bom has a production_line_code STRING field — a denormalized reference to the production line this BOM is used on. Replacing it with a proper FK normalizes the relationship. production_line']
  routing_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.routing. Business justification: production_bom has a routing_number STRING field — a denormalized reference to the routing associated with this BOM. In automotive manufacturing, a production BOM is paired with a routing (the BOM def']
  sku_master_id varchar [note: 'Reference identifier linking this manufacturing BOM (mBOM) to the originating engineering BOM (eBOM) in Siemens Teamcenter PLM or PTC Windchill. Enables traceability from shop floor execution back to the engineering design intent. The eBOM-to-mBOM transformation is managed via the PLM-SAP integration.']
  approval_date date [note: 'The date on which this manufacturing BOM received formal approval from the designated authority. Precedes the release_date in the BOM lifecycle workflow. Required for IATF 16949 documented information control.']
  approved_by varchar [note: 'User ID or employee identifier of the approving authority (e.g., Chief Engineer, Manufacturing Manager) who approved this BOM for production release. Distinct from released_by — approval may be a separate workflow step in Teamcenter PLM or SAP workflow.']
  apqp_phase varchar [note: 'Current APQP phase associated with this BOM\'s program lifecycle. Phase 1=Plan and Define, Phase 2=Product Design and Development, Phase 3=Process Design and Development, Phase 4=Product and Process Validation, Phase 5=Launch/Feedback. Tracks BOM maturity relative to the program development gate.']
  assembly_type varchar [note: 'Indicates whether the vehicle is assembled as a Completely Built Unit (CBU), Completely Knocked Down (CKD) kit for overseas assembly, or Semi Knocked Down (SKD) kit. Determines the BOM structure, component packaging, and applicable customs/import regulations for the target market.']
  base_quantity decimal(18,6) [note: 'The base quantity of the header material for which the BOM component quantities are defined (e.g., 1 vehicle). All component quantities in the BOM are expressed relative to this base quantity. Corresponds to the SAP BOM header base quantity field.']
  base_unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'The unit of measure for the BOM base quantity (e.g., \'EA\' for each/vehicle, \'PC\' for piece, \'SET\' for set). Corresponds to the SAP material master base unit of measure. Ensures consistent quantity calculations during MRP explosion and production order processing. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: EA|PC|KG|L|M|M2|M3|SET — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  bom_alternative varchar [note: 'SAP BOM alternative identifier (e.g., \'01\', \'02\') distinguishing multiple valid BOM configurations for the same material and plant. Supports variant BOMs for different production lines or regional configurations (e.g., LHD vs RHD, CKD vs SKD).']
  bom_description varchar [note: 'Human-readable description of the manufacturing BOM, typically including the vehicle model, model year, plant, and key configuration attributes (e.g., \'2025 F-150 XLT 3.5L EcoBoost AWD – Detroit Assembly Plant – MY2025 Production BOM\'). Used for display in SAP, Teamcenter PLM, and reporting tools.']
  bom_level varchar [note: 'The maximum depth/level of the BOM hierarchy for this manufacturing BOM (e.g., 1 = single-level, 5 = five levels deep). Used to understand BOM complexity and to control multi-level BOM explosion in MRP and production scheduling.']
  bom_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the manufacturing BOM. \'draft\' indicates the BOM is under engineering review. \'released\' means it has been authorized for production use. \'frozen\' indicates no further changes are permitted (e.g., during active production run). \'superseded\' means a newer BOM version has replaced it. \'obsolete\' means the BOM is no longer valid.']
  bom_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the BOM by its intended use in the manufacturing process. \'production\' is the standard mBOM used on the shop floor. \'phantom\' represents sub-assemblies that are not stocked. \'sales\' is used for variant configuration. \'spare_parts\' is for after-sales service. \'co_product\' is for by-products in manufacturing.']
  bom_usage varchar [note: 'SAP BOM usage indicator defining the organizational context in which the BOM is applied. \'production\' (SAP usage 1) is the standard for shop floor manufacturing. \'engineering\' (SAP usage 2) is for design/development. \'universal\' (SAP usage 3) applies across all usages. Determines which BOM is selected during MRP and production order processing.']
  bom_version varchar [note: 'Version identifier of the manufacturing BOM (e.g., \'v1.0\', \'v2.3\'). Incremented each time the BOM is revised through an approved Engineering Change Order. Enables version history tracking and rollback capability for quality investigations.']
  change_reason varchar [note: 'Free-text description of the business reason for creating or modifying this BOM version (e.g., \'Cost reduction – substitute supplier for part X\', \'Regulatory compliance – CARB Tier 3 emission update\', \'Quality improvement – NVH fix per TSB-2024-001\'). Required for IATF 16949 change control documentation.']
  component_count varchar [note: 'Total number of distinct component line items (BOM positions) in this manufacturing BOM. Provides a quick indicator of BOM complexity and is used for completeness validation during BOM release. Does not include phantom sub-assembly components.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this manufacturing BOM record was first created in the source system (SAP PP or Teamcenter PLM). Represents the business event of BOM creation, not the ETL load time. Formatted as yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX per enterprise data standards.']
  eco_number varchar [note: 'The Engineering Change Order (ECO) number that authorized the creation or last modification of this manufacturing BOM. Provides full traceability from the shop floor BOM back to the formal engineering change management process in Teamcenter PLM. Required for IATF 16949 change control compliance.']
  effectivity_end_date date [note: 'The date after which this manufacturing BOM version is no longer valid for production use. Corresponds to the SAP BOM \'Valid To\' date. Null indicates the BOM is open-ended with no planned expiry. Used to manage BOM transitions during model year changeovers and End of Production (EOP).']
  effectivity_start_date date [note: 'The date from which this manufacturing BOM version becomes effective and valid for production use. Corresponds to the SAP BOM \'Valid From\' date. Used for date-effective BOM explosion in MRP and production order creation. Aligns with Start of Production (SOP) planning.']
  eop_date date [note: 'The planned or actual End of Production (EOP) date for the vehicle program associated with this BOM. Marks the milestone when serial production of this vehicle configuration ceases. Used for last-time-buy planning, spare parts provisioning, and regulatory compliance archiving.']
  homologation_status varchar [note: 'Status of the regulatory homologation (type approval) for the vehicle configuration defined by this BOM in the target regulatory market. \'approved\' is required before vehicles built to this BOM can be legally sold in the target market. Managed through the Regulatory Compliance and Homologation process.']
  is_configurable boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this BOM uses SAP Variant Configuration (VC) to support multiple vehicle variants from a single configurable BOM structure. True = configurable BOM with characteristic-based component selection. False = fixed BOM for a single specific vehicle configuration.']
  is_phantom_assembly boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the header material of this BOM is a phantom assembly — a logical grouping of components that is not physically stocked or produced as a discrete unit. Phantom assemblies are \'exploded through\' during MRP and production order processing. Common for sub-assemblies in JIS manufacturing.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent modification to this manufacturing BOM record in the source system. Used to detect changes during incremental data loads into the Databricks Silver Layer and to support BOM change history auditing required by IATF 16949.']
  material_number varchar [note: 'SAP material number of the header material (finished vehicle or major assembly) for which this BOM is defined. This is the top-level material in the BOM hierarchy, corresponding to the vehicle configuration or assembly unit.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'The four-digit automotive Model Year (MY) for which this manufacturing BOM is valid (e.g., 2025). Determines the applicable regulatory standards, feature content, and homologation requirements. Aligns with NHTSA and EPA model year definitions.']
  powertrain_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the vehicle powertrain technology for which this BOM is defined. ICE = Internal Combustion Engine, HEV = Hybrid Electric Vehicle, PHEV = Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle, BEV = Battery Electric Vehicle, FCEV = Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle. Determines applicable emissions regulations (EPA, CARB, WLTP) and BOM component sets.']
  ppap_status varchar [note: 'Status of the Production Part Approval Process (PPAP) for the components in this BOM. PPAP is the AIAG/IATF 16949 process that verifies supplier components meet design and specification requirements before production launch. \'approved\' is required before the BOM can be released to production.']
  predecessor_bom_number varchar [note: 'BOM number of the previous manufacturing BOM version that this BOM supersedes. Enables backward navigation in the BOM version chain for change impact analysis and quality investigations. Null for the initial BOM version.']
  regulatory_market_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the target regulatory market for which this BOM is homologated (e.g., \'US-FEDERAL\', \'US-CARB\', \'EU-EURO6\', \'JP-JC08\', \'CN-CHINA6\'). Determines applicable emissions standards (EPA, CARB, Euro 6, WLTP), safety regulations (NHTSA FMVSS, UNECE), and CAFE compliance requirements.']
  release_date date [note: 'The date on which this manufacturing BOM was formally released to production (status changed to \'released\'). Marks the point at which the BOM is authorized for use in production orders and MRP planning. Corresponds to the SAP BOM release date.']
  released_by varchar [note: 'User ID or employee identifier of the manufacturing engineer or release authority who formally released this BOM to production in SAP PP or Teamcenter PLM. Required for audit trail and change control compliance under IATF 16949.']
  revision_level varchar [note: 'Alphanumeric revision level of the BOM (e.g., \'A\', \'B\', \'C\' or \'01\', \'02\'). Used in conjunction with bom_version to track incremental changes within a version. Aligns with SAP BOM revision level and Teamcenter PLM revision management.']
  sop_date date [note: 'The planned or actual Start of Production (SOP) date for the vehicle program associated with this BOM. Marks the milestone when serial production begins using this BOM configuration. Critical for program management, supplier readiness, and PPAP completion tracking.']
  superseded_by_bom_number varchar [note: 'BOM number of the newer manufacturing BOM that has replaced this one (populated when bom_status = \'superseded\'). Enables forward navigation in the BOM version chain for traceability and historical analysis. Null when this is the current active BOM.']
  vehicle_model_code varchar [note: 'Internal OEM code identifying the vehicle model (nameplate) for which this mBOM is defined (e.g., \'F150\', \'RAV4\', \'MODEL3\'). Used to associate the BOM with the correct vehicle program in PLM and SAP.']

  Note: 'Manufacturing Bill of Materials (BOM) record representing the production-level BOM used on the shop floor for a specific vehicle configuration and model year. Distinct from the engineering BOM (eBOM) in the engineering domain — this is the manufacturing BOM (mBOM) as released to production. Captures BOM header (vehicle model, MY, plant, effectivity dates), BOM status, and reference to the engineering change order that authorized it. Managed via SAP PP and Teamcenter PLM BOM transfer.'
}

Table manufacturing.material_consumption {
  material_consumption_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for each material consumption (goods issue) transaction record on the shop floor. Primary key of this table.']
  dealership_id varchar [note: 'Employee ID of the shop floor operator or MES user who confirmed or posted the material consumption. Used for accountability, audit trail, and operator-level quality traceability per IATF 16949.']
  inbound_part_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to supply.inbound_part. Business justification: Material consumption reports must reference the specific inbound part receipt to calculate usage rates and supplier performance metrics.']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant where the material consumption occurred. Aligns with SAP organizational unit for plant.']
  procurement_goods_receipt_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to procurement.procurement_goods_receipt. Business justification: IATF 16949 requires full part traceability from goods receipt through production consumption. This link enables 3-way match reconciliation, quantity variance analysis between received and consumed sto']
  production_bom_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.production_bom. Business justification: Material consumption records actual goods issues against a production BOM. The FK to production_bom normalizes the BOM reference. bom_version is a denormalized copy of production_bom.bom_version and c']
  production_order_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the production order against which this material was consumed. Links consumption to the manufacturing work order in SAP PP.']
  sku_master_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the material master record for the consumed part or component. Corresponds to the SAP Material Master (MM) object.']
  storage_location_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the storage location (bin, supermarket, line-side buffer) from which the material was issued for consumption.']
  vin_registry_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.vin_registry. Business justification: VIN-level material traceability is a regulatory and quality requirement in automotive — recall investigations and warranty root-cause analysis require knowing exactly which parts (by batch/serial) wer']
  work_center_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the work center (assembly station, stamping cell, paint booth, etc.) on the shop floor where the material was consumed.']
  assembly_station_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the specific assembly station or line position (JIS – Just-in-Sequence station) where the material was consumed. More granular than work center; used for shop floor traceability.']
  batch_number varchar [note: 'Supplier or internal batch/lot number of the consumed material. Critical for traceability, recall management, and IATF 16949 batch traceability requirements. Nullable for non-batch-managed materials.']
  bom_item_number varchar [note: 'The BOM line item number identifying the specific component position within the production BOM. Links the consumption record back to the engineering BOM structure in Siemens Teamcenter PLM.']
  consumption_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the material consumption record. \'posted\' = goods issue confirmed in SAP; \'reversed\' = goods issue reversed (movement type 262); \'blocked\' = held for quality review; \'pending\' = awaiting MES confirmation.']
  consumption_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The precise date and time when the material was physically issued from stock and consumed at the work center on the shop floor. This is the principal business event timestamp for this transaction.']
  cost_variance_amount decimal(18,4) [note: 'Monetary value of the consumption variance (actual cost minus planned cost) in plant currency. Positive = cost overrun; negative = cost saving. Key metric for production cost variance reporting in SAP CO.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this material consumption record was first created in the source system (SAP MM or MES). Audit trail field for data lineage and Silver layer ingestion tracking.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'ISO 4217 three-letter currency code for the material cost amount (e.g., USD, EUR, JPY). Supports multi-currency manufacturing operations across global plants.']
  goods_movement_number varchar [note: 'Externally visible SAP material document number generated when the goods issue (movement type 261) is posted. Serves as the business-facing transaction identifier traceable in SAP MM.']
  goods_movement_type varchar [note: 'SAP movement type code indicating the nature of the goods movement (e.g., 261 = goods issue to production order, 262 = reversal, 291 = goods issue to cost center). Drives inventory and cost accounting postings.']
  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent modification to this material consumption record in the source system. Used for incremental data load detection and audit trail in the Databricks Silver layer.']
  material_cost_amount decimal(18,4) [note: 'Standard cost value of the consumed material quantity at the time of goods issue, expressed in the plant currency. Calculated as quantity consumed × standard price from SAP Material Master. Feeds production cost accounting and variance reporting.']
  mes_transaction_code varchar [note: 'Unique transaction identifier from the Apriso/Dassault MES system that triggered or confirmed this material consumption event. Enables cross-system reconciliation between MES shop floor execution and SAP MM goods movements.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'The model year (MY) of the vehicle being assembled (e.g., \'2025\'). Used for model-year-level consumption analysis, BOM alignment, and regulatory compliance reporting.']
  operation_number varchar [note: 'The routing operation number within the production order at which the material was consumed (e.g., operation 0010 = body shop, 0020 = paint, 0030 = final assembly). Enables process-step-level consumption analysis.']
  original_goods_movement_number varchar [note: 'Reference to the original SAP material document number that this record reverses. Populated only when reversal_indicator is True. Enables audit trail for reversed consumption transactions.']
  planned_quantity decimal(18,6) [note: 'The planned quantity of this material as specified in the Bill of Materials (BOM) for the production order. Used to calculate consumption variance against actual quantity consumed.']
  posting_date date [note: 'The accounting posting date for the goods issue in SAP, which may differ from the physical consumption timestamp. Used for period-end cost accounting and production variance reporting.']
  powertrain_type varchar [note: 'Powertrain type of the vehicle being assembled: ICE (Internal Combustion Engine), HEV (Hybrid Electric Vehicle), PHEV (Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle), EV (Electric Vehicle), FCEV (Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle). Drives BOM variant selection and consumption expectations.']
  production_line_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the production line (e.g., \'LINE-A\', \'TRIM-2\', \'PAINT-EAST\') where the material was consumed. Used for line-level consumption analysis and capacity planning.']
  quality_inspection_lot varchar [note: 'SAP QM inspection lot number associated with the consumed material batch, if the material is subject to incoming or in-process quality inspection. Links consumption to quality results for IATF 16949 traceability.']
  quantity_consumed decimal(18,6) [note: 'Actual quantity of the material consumed (goods issued) against the production order. Expressed in the base unit of measure of the material. Core quantitative fact for this transaction.']
  quantity_variance decimal(18,6) [note: 'Difference between actual quantity consumed and planned BOM quantity (actual minus planned). Positive value indicates over-consumption; negative indicates under-consumption. Key input for production variance reporting and cost accounting.']
  reversal_indicator boolean [note: 'Flag indicating whether this consumption record is a reversal (cancellation) of a previously posted goods issue. True = this is a reversal document; False = original consumption posting.']
  scrap_indicator boolean [note: 'Flag indicating whether the consumed material was scrapped (True) rather than incorporated into the vehicle assembly. Scrap consumption is tracked separately for waste reporting, IATF 16949 scrap analysis, and environmental compliance.']
  scrap_reason_code varchar [note: 'Coded reason for material scrap when scrap_indicator is True (e.g., \'DAMAGE\', \'DEFECT\', \'WRONG_PART\', \'PROCESS_LOSS\'). Used for scrap root cause analysis and PPM defect rate reporting. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: DAMAGE|DEFECT|WRONG_PART|PROCESS_LOSS|HANDLING_ERROR|SUPPLIER_DEFECT|OTHER — promote to reference product]']
  serial_number varchar [note: 'Individual serial number of the consumed component where serial number management is active (e.g., ECU, battery module, transmission). Enables unit-level traceability for safety-critical parts. Nullable for non-serialized materials.']
  shift_code varchar [note: 'Production shift during which the material was consumed (DAY, AFTERNOON, NIGHT). Used for shift-level consumption analysis, variance trending, and workforce productivity reporting.']
  source_system_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the operational system of record from which this consumption record originated (SAP_MM = SAP S/4HANA MM goods movement, MES_APRISO = Apriso/Dassault MES, SAP_PP = SAP Production Planning, MANUAL = manual entry). Supports data lineage in the lakehouse.']
  supplier_number varchar [note: 'SAP vendor/supplier number of the supplier who provided the consumed material. Used for supplier-level consumption analysis, PPAP traceability, and supply chain variance reporting.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'Base unit of measure for the consumed quantity (e.g., EA = each, KG = kilogram, L = litre, M = metre, PC = piece). Aligns with SAP base UoM from Material Master. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: EA|KG|L|M|M2|M3|PC|SET|ROL|BOX — 10 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  variance_percentage decimal(10,4) [note: 'Quantity variance expressed as a percentage of the planned BOM quantity ((actual - planned) / planned × 100). Used for threshold-based alerting and PPM (Parts Per Million) defect rate analysis.']
  vehicle_model_code varchar [note: 'Internal OEM code identifying the vehicle model or platform being assembled (e.g., \'F150\', \'CAMRY-HEV\', \'SILVERADO-EV\'). Used for model-level consumption aggregation and BOM variance analysis.']
  wip_order_sequence varchar [note: 'Sequential position of this consumption event within the production order\'s WIP (Work in Progress) processing sequence. Used for JIS (Just-in-Sequence) ordering analysis and shop floor flow traceability.']

  Note: 'Transactional record capturing actual material consumption (goods issue) against a production order on the shop floor. Tracks consumed part number, quantity consumed, unit of measure, consumption timestamp, work center, production order reference, storage location, batch number, and variance from planned BOM quantity. Feeds SAP MM goods movement and cost accounting for production variance reporting.'
}

Table manufacturing.capacity_plan {
  capacity_plan_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique surrogate identifier for each capacity plan record in the manufacturing domain. Primary key for the capacity_plan data product.']
  bottleneck_work_center_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the work center identified as the capacity bottleneck constraining overall line throughput during this planning period. Used to prioritize capacity improvement investments and focus S&OP constraint management.']
  fleet_contract_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to sales.fleet_contract. Business justification: Large fleet contracts (government/national accounts) with high committed_volume directly trigger capacity planning decisions — headcount, shifts, capex investments. Capacity planners must reference th']
  model_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to vehicle.model. Business justification: Capacity planning in automotive is performed per vehicle model per plant/line. Model-level capacity reports, S&OP processes, and launch readiness reviews all require linking capacity plans to the vehi']
  organization_account_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to customer.customer_fleet_account. Business justification: Fleet renewal contracts (e.g., 2,000-unit rental fleet order) are primary demand signals that trigger capacity plan adjustments. Automotive capacity planners must link capacity plans to fleet accounts']
  plant_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the manufacturing plant for which this capacity plan is defined. Links to the manufacturing.plant master record.']
  production_line_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific production line (e.g., final assembly line, body shop line, paint line) within the plant for which capacity is being planned. Links to the manufacturing.work_center or production line master.']
  production_schedule_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to manufacturing.production_schedule. Business justification: Capacity plans are developed in response to production schedules — the planned production volumes in the schedule drive the capacity requirements analysis. This FK links the capacity plan to the sched']
  approval_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the capacity plan was formally approved by the designated authority. Marks the transition from \'submitted\' to \'approved\' status in the S&OP governance process.']
  approved_by varchar [note: 'Name or employee identifier of the S&OP or manufacturing leadership authority who approved this capacity plan. Required for audit trail and governance compliance under IATF 16949.']
  available_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Total scheduled production hours available in the planning period after accounting for planned downtime, shift breaks, and scheduled maintenance. Used in conjunction with rated_capacity_jph to compute rated capacity in units.']
  bottleneck_constraint_description varchar [note: 'Narrative description of the primary capacity constraint at the bottleneck work center (e.g., \'Paint oven cycle time limits throughput to 58 JPH\', \'Robotic welding cell maintenance window reduces available hours\'). Supports root cause analysis and CapEx justification.']
  capacity_gap_units varchar [note: 'Difference between planned_production_units and demonstrated_capacity_units, representing the capacity shortfall or surplus for the planning period. Positive value indicates a capacity gap requiring corrective action (overtime, additional shifts, CapEx); negative value indicates surplus capacity.']
  capacity_plan_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the capacity planning methodology used. \'rough_cut\' (RCCP — Rough Cut Capacity Planning) for high-level S&OP; \'detailed\' for work-center-level CRP (Capacity Requirements Planning); \'constraint_based\' for Theory of Constraints analysis; \'what_if\' for scenario planning and CapEx evaluation.']
  capacity_utilization_percent decimal(5,2) [note: 'Planned capacity utilization rate expressed as a percentage, calculated as (planned_production_units / rated_capacity_units) × 100. Drives S&OP decisions on overtime, additional shifts, and capital investment. Target thresholds are typically 85–95% for automotive assembly.']
  capex_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'Estimated capital expenditure amount required to address the capacity gap identified in this plan. Expressed in the plant\'s operating currency. Used for CapEx budget submissions and investment approval processes.']
  capex_required boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this capacity plan identifies a requirement for capital investment (CapEx) to close a capacity gap (e.g., new tooling, additional robots, facility expansion). Triggers CapEx approval workflow in SAP FI/CO.']
  changeover_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Hours allocated for model changeovers, tooling changes, and line reconfigurations during the planning period. Relevant for mixed-model assembly lines producing multiple vehicle variants (e.g., ICE and BEV on the same line).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this capacity plan record was first created in the system. Provides the audit trail creation anchor for data lineage and compliance purposes.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'ISO 4217 three-letter currency code for all monetary values in this capacity plan record (e.g., USD, EUR, MXN, CNY). Ensures consistent financial reporting across multi-plant, multi-currency manufacturing operations.']
  daily_production_target varchar [note: 'Planned number of vehicles to be produced per working day during the planning period, derived from planned_production_units / working_days. Used for daily MES scheduling and shift-level production targets.']
  demonstrated_capacity_units varchar [note: 'Actual production output achieved in a comparable prior period, used as the realistic baseline for capacity planning. Reflects real-world constraints such as quality losses, minor stoppages, and changeovers. Key input to APQP (Advanced Product Quality Planning) capacity studies.']
  eop_date date [note: 'Planned End of Production (EOP) date for the vehicle model or program covered by this capacity plan. Marks the milestone when production of the model is scheduled to cease. Used for capacity wind-down planning and asset redeployment decisions.']
  fiscal_year varchar [note: 'Fiscal year (FY) to which this capacity plan belongs, used for CapEx (Capital Expenditure) alignment, budget planning, and financial reporting. Format: FY followed by 4-digit year (e.g., FY2025).']
  headcount_required varchar [note: 'Total direct labor headcount required to execute the planned production volume during the planning period. Used for workforce planning, hiring decisions, and labor cost budgeting in SuccessFactors and SAP CO.']
  is_bottleneck_constrained boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the capacity plan is constrained by a bottleneck work center that limits overall line throughput below the planned production target. When True, the bottleneck_work_center_id and bottleneck_constraint_description fields are populated.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'Automotive model year (MY) for which this capacity plan is prepared. Capacity plans are typically aligned to MY production programs, SOP (Start of Production), and EOP (End of Production) milestones.']
  number_of_shifts varchar [note: 'Number of production shifts planned per working day for this capacity plan (e.g., 1, 2, or 3 shifts). Directly determines available production hours and total capacity for the planning period.']
  plan_end_date date [note: 'Last calendar date of the planning period covered by this capacity plan record. Together with plan_start_date, defines the planning window for capacity supply and demand balancing.']
  plan_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known, human-readable business identifier for the capacity plan record, used in S&OP (Sales and Operations Planning) communications, SAP PP capacity planning transactions (CM01/CM21), and cross-functional reporting. Format: CP-{PLANT}-{YEAR}-{MONTH}-{SEQ}.']
  plan_start_date date [note: 'First calendar date of the planning period covered by this capacity plan record. Used to align capacity supply with demand signals from S&OP and MRP (Material Requirements Planning) runs.']
  plan_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the capacity plan record. \'draft\' indicates work-in-progress; \'submitted\' indicates pending approval; \'approved\' indicates sign-off by S&OP leadership; \'active\' indicates the operative plan driving production scheduling; \'superseded\' indicates replaced by a newer version; \'archived\' indicates historical record.']
  plan_version varchar [note: 'Version identifier of the capacity plan, enabling management of multiple planning iterations (e.g., v1.0, v2.0) within the same planning period. Supports S&OP cycle versioning and plan-over-plan variance analysis.']
  planned_downtime_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Total planned downtime hours for the production line during the planning period, including scheduled maintenance, tooling changeovers, model changeovers, and planned shutdowns. Reduces available production hours from the theoretical maximum.']
  planned_maintenance_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Subset of planned downtime hours specifically allocated to preventive and predictive maintenance activities on production equipment. Tracked separately from changeover downtime for maintenance KPI reporting and SAP PM integration.']
  planned_overtime_hours decimal(10,2) [note: 'Total overtime hours planned for the production line during the planning period to meet production targets beyond standard shift capacity. Feeds into workforce cost planning and labor budget in SAP CO.']
  planned_production_units varchar [note: 'Number of vehicles planned for production in the planning period as agreed in the S&OP process. Represents the demand signal that capacity must satisfy. Compared against rated and demonstrated capacity to identify gaps.']
  planning_period_type varchar [note: 'Granularity of the capacity planning horizon for this record. \'weekly\' supports short-term MES scheduling; \'monthly\' supports S&OP cycles; \'quarterly\' and \'annual\' support CapEx and strategic workforce planning.']
  planning_scenario varchar [note: 'Demand scenario underpinning this capacity plan. \'base\' reflects the consensus S&OP forecast; \'optimistic\' reflects upside demand; \'pessimistic\' reflects downside risk; \'stretch\' reflects maximum achievable output. Enables scenario comparison for executive decision-making.']
  powertrain_type varchar [note: 'Powertrain technology type planned for production in this capacity plan. ICE = Internal Combustion Engine; HEV = Hybrid Electric Vehicle; PHEV = Plug-in Hybrid Electric Vehicle; BEV = Battery Electric Vehicle; FCEV = Fuel Cell Electric Vehicle. Impacts tooling, line configuration, and capacity requirements.']
  rated_capacity_jph decimal(10,2) [note: 'Theoretical maximum production rate expressed in Jobs Per Hour (JPH) for the production line under ideal conditions. Derived from takt time and line design specifications. Used as the baseline for capacity utilization calculations in S&OP.']
  rated_capacity_units varchar [note: 'Theoretical maximum number of vehicles that can be produced in the planning period, calculated as rated_capacity_jph × available_hours. Represents the upper bound of production capacity for S&OP and CapEx planning.']
  sap_capacity_plan_code varchar [note: 'Source system identifier for this capacity plan record in SAP S/4HANA PP (Production Planning) module, used for data lineage traceability and reconciliation between the lakehouse Silver layer and the operational ERP system.']
  sop_date date [note: 'Planned Start of Production (SOP) date for the vehicle model or program covered by this capacity plan. Marks the milestone when full-rate production is scheduled to begin. Critical for capacity ramp-up planning and APQP milestone tracking.']
  takt_time_seconds varchar [note: 'Planned takt time in seconds for the production line during this planning period, representing the rate at which vehicles must be produced to meet customer demand. Takt time = available production time / planned production units. Core metric in JIS (Just-in-Sequence) and line balancing.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this capacity plan record was last modified. Tracks plan revisions through the S&OP cycle and supports change management auditing.']
  working_days varchar [note: 'Number of scheduled working days in the planning period after excluding weekends, public holidays, and planned plant shutdowns. Used to compute daily production targets and available hours.']

  Note: 'Master record for plant and line capacity planning, capturing planned production capacity by plant, production line, shift pattern, and planning period (weekly/monthly). Includes rated capacity (JPH × available hours), demonstrated capacity, capacity utilization percentage, bottleneck work center, planned overtime hours, and capacity plan version. Supports S&OP (Sales and Operations Planning) and capital investment decisions.'
}

Business justification: The routing table has a work_center STRING field that is a denormalized reference to the primary/default work center for this routing. Replacing it with a proper FK to work_center normalizes this rela']

  version_number varchar [note: 'Incremental version number for change management of the routing.']
  created_by varchar [note: 'User identifier who originally created the routing.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for routing. Referenced by routing_id.'
}

  Note: 'Master record for every purchased part number sourced from external suppliers.   temperature_control_required boolean [note: 'True if the shipment must be kept within a temperature range.']

  temperature_min_c decimal(5,2) [note: 'Lower bound of the required temperature range for the shipment.']
  total_volume_m3 decimal(12,3) [note: 'Aggregate volume of the shipment, expressed in cubic meters.']
  total_weight_kg decimal(12,3) [note: 'Aggregate weight of all items in the shipment, expressed in kilograms.']

  Note: 'Tracks an inbound shipment of parts from a supplier plant to an OEM receiving dock. Captures ASN (Advance Shipping Notice) number, carrier, mode of transport (road, rail, air, sea), departure date/time, estimated arrival date/time, actual arrival date/time, total weight, total volume, number of containers/pallets, customs declaration number, and shipment status (in transit, arrived, cleared, received). Integrates with SAP MM inbound delivery (VL31N).'
}

  Note: 'Individual line item within a purchase order, representing a specific material, service, or part number being procured.   gauge_type varchar [note: 'Specific gauge or instrument type used.']
  inspection_category varchar [note: 'Broad category of the inspection activity.']
  inspection_plan_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the inspection plan.']
  is_automated boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the inspection is performed by automated equipment.']
  last_review_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent plan review.']
  measurement_method varchar [note: 'Technique used to perform the measurement.']
  measurement_unit varchar [note: 'Unit of measure for tolerances and results.']
  model_year varchar [note: 'Model year (calendar year) of the vehicle.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free‑form comments or special instructions.']
  plan_name varchar [note: 'Human‑readable name of the inspection plan.']
  plan_number varchar [note: 'Business identifier assigned to the inspection plan.']
  plan_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the plan (e.g., incoming material, in‑process, final vehicle).']
  plant_location varchar [note: 'Code of the manufacturing plant where the plan is executed.']
  regulatory_compliance varchar [note: 'Applicable regulatory framework(s) for the inspection.']
  requires_approval boolean [note: 'True if the inspection results must be formally approved.']
  review_cycle_days varchar [note: 'Number of days between mandatory reviews.']
  revision_number varchar [note: 'Sequential revision number for change tracking.']
  sample_size varchar [note: 'Number of units to be inspected per batch.']
  tolerance_lower decimal(10,4) [note: 'Minimum acceptable measurement value.']
  tolerance_upper decimal(10,4) [note: 'Maximum acceptable measurement value.']
  updated_by varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user who last modified the inspection plan.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the inspection plan.']
  version varchar [note: 'Version label of the inspection plan (e.g., v1.0, v2.1).']
  created_by varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user who created the inspection plan.']

  Note: 'Detailed inspection plan specifying the characteristics to be measured, measurement methods, gauges/instruments, tolerances, sample sizes, and acceptance criteria for incoming material, in-process, and final vehicle inspections. Supports incoming material inspection (IQC), in-process quality gates, and PDI (Pre-Delivery Inspection). Linked to SAP QM inspection lots.'
}

  Note: 'Quality system and process audit record capturing planned and unplanned audits conducted at plants, supplier facilities, or dealer service centers. Supports IATF 16949 internal audit requirements and customer-specific requirements (CSR).'
}

Table quality.characteristic {
  characteristic_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for characteristic']
  parent_characteristic_id varchar [note: 'Self-referencing FK on characteristic (parent_characteristic_id)']
  category varchar [note: 'Broad classification of the characteristic within quality domains.']
  characteristic_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the characteristic.']
  created_by_user varchar [note: 'User identifier who initially created the characteristic record.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the characteristic record was first created.']
  criticality_level varchar [note: 'Business impact rating of the characteristic on product quality.']
  data_type varchar [note: 'Data type of the characteristic\'s measured value.']
  description varchar [note: 'Detailed description of what the characteristic measures or represents.']
  effective_from date [note: 'Date when the characteristic becomes valid for use.']
  effective_until date [note: 'Date when the characteristic is retired or superseded (nullable).']
  frequency varchar [note: 'How often the characteristic is measured or evaluated.']
  measurement_method varchar [note: 'Technique used to capture the characteristic value.']
  measurement_unit varchar [note: 'Unit of measure associated with the characteristic (e.g., mm, kg, sec).']
  name varchar [note: 'Human‑readable name of the quality characteristic.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free‑form comments or observations about the characteristic.']
  target_value decimal(12,4) [note: 'Target or nominal value that the characteristic should achieve.']
  tolerance_lower decimal(12,4) [note: 'Maximum acceptable deviation below the target value.']
  tolerance_upper decimal(12,4) [note: 'Maximum acceptable deviation above the target value.']
  updated_by_user varchar [note: 'User identifier who last modified the characteristic record.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the characteristic record.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for characteristic. Referenced by characteristic_id.'
}

Table quality.root_cause_analysis {
  root_cause_analysis_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for root_cause_analysis']
  defect_record_id varchar [note: 'add column defect_record_id (BIGINT) with FK to quality.defect_record.defect_record_id - root cause analysis is performed on specific defects']
  parent_root_cause_analysis_id varchar [note: 'Self-referencing FK on root_cause_analysis (related_root_cause_analysis_id)']
  actual_resolution_date date [note: 'Date on which the corrective action was actually completed.']
  category varchar [note: 'High‑level classification of the root cause source.']
  code varchar [note: 'Business identifier or code used to reference the root cause in reports and work orders.']
  corrective_action_owner varchar [note: 'Person or team accountable for executing the corrective action plan.']
  corrective_action_plan varchar [note: 'Planned corrective actions to eliminate or mitigate the root cause.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the root cause analysis record was first created.']
  description varchar [note: 'Detailed narrative describing the root cause, its symptoms, and context.']
  detection_phase varchar [note: 'Process phase in which the root cause was first detected.']
  effective_from date [note: 'Date from which the root cause analysis definition becomes active.']
  effective_until date [note: 'Date after which the root cause analysis definition is retired (nullable).']
  failure_mode varchar [note: 'Specific failure mode linked to the root cause (e.g., "Crack in chassis").']
  impact_description varchar [note: 'Narrative of the business or safety impact caused by the root cause.']
  name varchar [note: 'Human‑readable name of the root cause (e.g., "Incorrect Torque Specification").']
  occurrence_phase varchar [note: 'Process phase where the root cause actually occurred.']
  owner varchar [note: 'Name of the person or team responsible for investigating and resolving the root cause.']
  priority varchar [note: 'Business priority assigned to address the root cause.']
  risk_rating varchar [note: 'Overall risk rating associated with the root cause.']
  root_cause_analysis_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the root cause analysis.']
  root_cause_analysis_type varchar [note: 'Indicates whether the cause is systemic, occasional, or a one‑off event.']
  severity_level varchar [note: 'Numeric rating (1‑5) of the impact severity of the root cause on product quality.']
  target_resolution_date date [note: 'Planned date by which the corrective action should be completed.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the root cause analysis record.']
  verification_method varchar [note: 'Method used to verify that the corrective action resolved the root cause.']
  verification_result varchar [note: 'Outcome of the verification activity.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for root_cause_analysis. Referenced by root_cause_analysis_id.'
}

Table quality.inspection_characteristic {
  inspection_characteristic_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for the inspection characteristic association record.']
  characteristic_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to the characteristic being measured or inspected.']
  inspection_plan_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to the inspection plan that defines the overall inspection scope and context.']
  acceptance_criteria varchar [note: 'Pass/fail decision rules specific to this characteristic within this inspection plan (e.g., AQL levels, Cpk requirements).']
  created_by_user varchar [note: 'User identifier of the quality engineer who added this characteristic to the inspection plan.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this inspection characteristic requirement was added to the plan.']
  frequency varchar [note: 'How often this characteristic should be inspected under this plan (e.g., every unit, hourly, per lot).']
  gauge_type varchar [note: 'Specific gauge or measurement instrument required for inspecting this characteristic under this plan.']
  is_critical boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this characteristic is designated as critical within the context of this inspection plan.']
  measurement_method varchar [note: 'Specific measurement technique to be used for this characteristic within this inspection plan context. May override or specialize the default method defined on the characteristic master.']
  sample_size varchar [note: 'Number of samples to be measured for this characteristic during inspection execution per this plan.']
  sequence_number varchar [note: 'Order in which this characteristic should be inspected within the inspection plan workflow.']
  tolerance_lower decimal(12,4) [note: 'Plan-specific lower tolerance limit for this characteristic. May override the characteristic master tolerance based on application context.']
  tolerance_upper decimal(12,4) [note: 'Plan-specific upper tolerance limit for this characteristic. May override the characteristic master tolerance based on application context.']
  updated_by_user varchar [note: 'User identifier who last modified this inspection characteristic requirement.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent modification to this inspection characteristic requirement.']

  Note: 'This association product represents the inspection requirement between an inspection plan and a characteristic. It captures the specific measurement instructions, tolerances, sampling rules, and acceptance criteria that apply when a particular characteristic is inspected under a particular inspection plan. Each record links one inspection_plan to one characteristic with attributes that exist only in the context of this inspection requirement.. Existence Justification: In automotive quality management (IATF 16949 / VDA standards), an inspection plan is explicitly structured as a collection of multiple characteristics to be inspected, and each characteristic can appear in multiple inspection plans (e.g., \'bore diameter\' is inspected in incoming material plans, in-process plans, and final vehicle PDI plans). The business actively manages \'inspection characteristics\' or \'inspection plan lines\' as operational entities with plan-specific measurement instructions, tolerances, sampling rules, and acceptance criteria that vary by context.'
}

Table logistics.carrier {

  state varchar [note: 'State or province of the carrier\'s primary business address.']
  tax_identifier varchar [note: 'Government‑issued tax identifier for the carrier.']

  transport_modes varchar [note: 'Transport modes the carrier is authorized to operate.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time of the most recent update to the carrier record.']
  website varchar [note: 'Public website URL of the carrier.']

  Note: 'Master record for transport carriers (road haulers, rail operators, ocean shipping lines, port logistics providers) engaged for finished vehicle and CKD/SKD kit logistics. Captures carrier legal name, SCAC code, DOT number, transport modes supported, operating regions, contract reference, insurance certificate details, IATF 16949 compliance status, carrier tier classification, and performance rating. SSOT for carrier identity within the logistics domain. Referenced by transport orders, shipment legs, freight invoices, damage claims, and rate contracts.'
}

Table logistics.vehicle_compound {
  vehicle_compound_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the vehicle compound (yard, port, or regional staging area).']

  country_code varchar [note: 'Three‑letter ISO country code where the compound resides.']

  current_occupancy varchar [note: 'Current number of vehicles stored in the compound.']

  longitude decimal(9,6) [note: 'Geographic longitude of the compound (decimal degrees).']
  manager_email varchar [note: 'Contact email address for the compound operator/manager.']
  manager_phone varchar [note: 'Contact phone number for the compound operator/manager.']
  name varchar [note: 'Human‑readable name of the storage compound.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free‑form notes or remarks about the compound.']
  operator_name varchar [note: 'Name of the person or organization responsible for day‑to‑day operations of the compound.']
  otd_target_percentage decimal(5,2) [note: 'Target percentage for on‑time delivery performance for shipments originating from this compound.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal/ZIP code of the compound.']
  region_code varchar [note: 'Internal code representing the broader geographic region (e.g., NA, EU, APAC).']
  security_level varchar [note: 'Security classification of the compound (low, medium, high).']
  state_province varchar [note: 'State or province of the compound location.']
  storage_area_sqft decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total floor area of the compound in square feet.']
  temperature_controlled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the compound maintains temperature‑controlled storage.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the compound record.']
  vehicle_compound_status varchar [note: 'Current operational status of the compound.']
  waste_handling_capability boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the compound is equipped to handle hazardous waste.']

  Note: 'Master record for vehicle storage compounds, yards, and staging areas used in the outbound logistics network (plant yards, rail yards, port compounds, regional distribution centers). Captures compound name, location, type (plant/port/regional/dealer prep), storage capacity (units), current occupancy, operator, PDI capability flag, and compound status. Enables compound capacity planning and in-transit inventory tracking.'
}
